No schema change, no migration, no new API route, no permission change, no new dependency. **NEW CORE PUCK BLOCK `MobileBar`** ("Mobile Bar"), registered in the Site category of BOTH `headerPuckConfig` and `footerPuckConfig` (either renders on every page; neither is better than the other). Its cells come from an `items` array field, each `{kind, label, icon, href, newTab, menuId, sheetHeading, moduleItemId}` where kind is `link` / `menu` / `account` / `module`. Puck array rows share one field set, so the kind-specific fields are LABELLED by kind ("Link cells: web address") rather than hidden. **NEW FILES:** `lib/puck/mobileBar.ts` (client-safe contract + `mobileBarCss(barId, props)`, the whole stylesheet, plus `ModuleMobileBarItemProps`), `lib/puck/components/MobileBarIcons.tsx` (14 icons + `MODULE_SLOT_ICON`), `lib/puck/components/MobileBarClient.tsx` (the one client island: cells, current-page highlight, the menu panel), `lib/puck/mobile-bar-items.ts` (server resolver for the extension point), `lib/puck/mobile-bar-point.ts` (the point name alone - `mobile-bar-items.ts` reaches the database and `config.core.tsx` is client-safe by contract), `lib/puck/mobileBar.test.ts` (5 cases). **VISIBILITY IS THE LOAD-BEARING BIT:** every declaration the bar emits - including `body{padding-bottom}` and the new `:root{--cactus-bottom-bar-offset}` - is emitted INSIDE the breakpoint query the owner chose (`barOn`: phones / phones+tablets / all), so a phones-only bar adds nothing whatever to a desktop render. Painting a base style and hiding it again would have left every desktop page with a strip of dead space. **THE MODULE SEAM: new public extension point `core.mobile-bar-items`.** A module's cell is a client component taking `{label, iconSize, showLabels}` and rendering ONE `<button>` using core's published classes (`cmb-item`/`cmb-icon`/`cmb-label`/`cmb-badge`); core draws no chrome round it, so a contributed cell and a core one match with nothing coordinated. Resolved server-side from installed manifests (`getModuleMobileBarItems`, same shape as `getShopCartHeaderActions`); a cell whose module is not installed gets no slot and the island DROPS it rather than leaving a hole in the grid. **THE EDITOR PICKER PROBLEM, and the third generated file:** the editor needs a list of available module cells, but `lib/modules/extension-points.public.ts` carries the components themselves and importing it from `config.core.tsx` would put every module's public components into every public page's bundle - the exact leak the public/full split exists to stop. So `scripts/generate-module-extension-points.mjs` now writes a THIRD file, `lib/modules/extension-points.meta.ts` (gitignored alongside the other two): `{point, id, label, moduleName, adminOnly}` and `publicEntriesForPoint(point)`, data only, no imports, safe anywhere. It lists a BUILD fact, so the picker may offer a module this site has not installed; the renderer asks the database and draws nothing. **RSC/EDITOR SPLIT:** `mobileBarItemsFor(props, isEditing)` is exported from `config.core.tsx` and shared; the editor half renders dashed placeholders for module cells and forces `barOn: 'all'` (`forceVisible`) so the bar is selectable on a desktop-width canvas; the published half is a new async `MobileBarWithModuleCells` in `config.rsc.tsx` that fills the slots, rendered as an ELEMENT by a thin sync `MobileBarRsc` because `wrapResponsiveRender` calls a render function directly and must get a plain element back (same reason `SiteLogoModuleLayoutRsc` exists). **`resolveTemplateData`** gains a `MobileBar` branch: `resolvedMenus` (an array PARALLEL to `items`, index is the join - Puck array rows have no stable id) and `accountHref` (`/{getMemberAreaPath()}` or its `/login`, decided server-side because only the server knows whether this visitor is signed in). **HEADER ROOT `sticky` GAINS A THIRD VALUE, `partial`** ("Sticky (top part scrolls away)"), with a responsive `scrollAway` field shown only for it. Implementation is one declaration, no scroll listener: `position:sticky` with a NEGATIVE `top` scrolls up by that much and then pins, so "how much scrolls away" is literally the number typed. Desktop is inline, tablet/mobile are a new `headerTopCss` media rule, and `--cactus-header-offset` now publishes `calc(height - scrollAway)` so sticky blocks below clear the part that STAYS. `headerOffsetCss` gate widened from `=== 'yes'` to `!== 'no'`. The `SiteHeader` block's own header element is untouched - it has no partial mode. **SHOP (`modules/shop`):** `drawerSide` gains `'bottom'` - full width, up from the bottom edge, 16px top corners, `max-height:min(85vh, 100vh - 2.5rem)`, its own `scd-slide-up` keyframe and reduced-motion arm; it overrides the side panel's width and top edge only, so head/body/foot/lines/buttons are byte-identical. NEW `modules/shop/components/public/CartMobileBarItem.tsx` (`shop-basket`): live count off `getCart`/`subscribeCart`, opens the same lazily-loaded `CartDrawerClient` with `DRAWER_DEFAULTS` + `drawerSide: 'bottom'` - a bar cell has no settings panel of its own, which is the trade for a cell needing no setup. **LIVE-CHAT (`modules/live-chat`):** NEW `lib/open-event.ts` (`LIVE_CHAT_OPEN_EVENT`) and `components/ChatMobileBarItem.tsx` (`live-chat-open`), which fires that event rather than touching `$chatwoot` - so a first press still goes through boot, the consent gate and Turnstile in `WidgetLoader`, which now listens for it. The bubble and its consent notice have their `bottom` rewritten to `calc(… + var(--cactus-bottom-bar-offset, 0px))` so they lift clear of a phone bar without this module knowing whether the site has one. **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint .` clean, `npx vitest run` **5991 passed / 0 failed / 376 skipped**. No SQL touched anywhere, so the backup round-trip and ledger-guard suites do not apply. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. Wiki: `Appearance-and-design.md` gains **Sticky, but only the bottom of it** and **A bottom bar for phones**; `Authoring-a-module.md` gains the `core.mobile-bar-items` row; `Shop.md` gains **Up from the bottom, full width**; `Live-Chat.md` gains the bar-cell bullet.)

Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**Cookie categories can be put in the order visitors should read them, with Necessary nailed to the top** - core only, uncommitted working tree. No schema change, no migration, no new route, no permission change, no new dependency, no new file. **THE GAP:** `ConsentBannerConfig.categories` has always been an ordered array and both public surfaces have always rendered it in array order, so the order was already load-bearing - there was simply no way to set it. New categories landed on the end (**Add category** and the module suggestion pills both `[...cats, …]`), and nothing anywhere held `necessary` at the front. **`lib/consent/types.ts` gains** `NECESSARY_CATEGORY_KEY` and the pure `withNecessaryFirst(categories)` - hoists the necessary row to index 0, returns the input untouched when it is already there or absent. Placed beside the type rather than in `client.ts` because `client.ts` is `'use client'` and the config API needs it too. **`app/api/admin/config/route.ts`** normalises rather than rejects: `withNecessaryFirst(incomingConsent.categories)` is what gets stored, so a hand-written payload burying necessary halfway down is corrected. The existing `hasNecessary` guard now compares against the constant. **`bumpConsentVersions` deliberately untouched** - it compares key SETS and per-key `required`/`defaultOn`/`label`/`description`, never positions, so a pure reorder bumps neither `categoriesVersion` nor `copyVersion` and re-prompts nobody. That was already true and is the behaviour that matters here. **`components/consent/ConsentBanner.tsx` and `ConsentPreferencesPanel.tsx`** each render a `useMemo`'d `orderedCategories` instead of `config.categories` - defensive, for config saved before the order was settable. Set operations (`noticeOnly`, `adjustable`, `buildDefaultDecision`, accept/reject builders) still read `config.categories`; order is irrelevant to all of them. **`app/cactus-admin/config/ConfigPageClient.tsx`**: `cats` is now `withNecessaryFirst(...)`, a new leading grid column (`'auto 0.67fr 0.67fr 2.67fr auto auto'`) carries `btn btn-ghost btn-sm` up/down arrows per row, and `moveCategory(index, dir)` swaps neighbours with a single `target < 1` bound - since necessary is pinned at index 0 that one check blocks both moving it and moving anything above it. Row 0's arrows and row 1's up arrow are disabled, titled "Necessary stays at the top". Header cell for the column is an `sr-only` "Order". Hint copy under **Cookie categories** rewritten to say the rest appear in the order arranged, and that reordering does not re-prompt. **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint .` clean. No SQL, so no live suite applies; no consent tests exist to run. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. Wiki `Configuration-reference.md` **Cookie consent banner**: the categories row now says "in the order visitors see them" and "pinned to the top", plus a new **Category order** paragraph.)

Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**Pictures carried inside a message were never resolved, so every one of them was an empty box** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. **THE DEFECT:** a signature logo written in Outlook, or a screenshot pasted into a sentence, is an ordinary attachment the markup points at by its `Content-ID` - `<img src="cid:image001.png@01D9">`. Nothing in this module resolved that, and nothing could have: the sync path never recorded the Content-ID at all, so the two halves were both stored and could not be put back together. `blockRemoteImages` deliberately leaves `cid:` alone (`isRemote` is false for it, correctly), and `messageDocumentCsp`'s `img-src` matches nothing shaped like a `cid:` URL, so the reading frame drew a broken box - under a note in `MessageBody.tsx` telling the reader that anything carried inside the message was already there. Two messages on the live Deskwell database carry such references today. **NEW MODULE MIGRATION `migrations/048_inline_images.sql`:** `uin_attachments.content_id TEXT` plus a partial index on `("message_id", "content_id") WHERE "content_id" IS NOT NULL`. Idempotent, TEXT only, so the backup schema-coverage backstop needs no new branch. **NEW ROUTE `app/api/messages/[id]/inline/[attachmentId]/route.ts`** (GET, `unifiedinbox.view` + `canOpenThread`): serves one inline picture's bytes **inline**, which the download route at `attachments/[id]` deliberately never does - that is why this is a route of its own rather than a flag on that one. Safe only because of the pair that follows it: a strict picture-type allow-list (**no SVG** - markup with script in it) and `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`. The attachment is checked to belong to the message that was named, so holding one id opens nothing else. `Cache-Control: private, max-age=300`, `maxDuration = 60` (the bytes may still be on the mail server). **NEW `lib/inline-images.ts`:** `DISPLAYABLE_IMAGE_TYPES` / `isDisplayableImageType` (now the ONE allow-list - `lib/remote-images.ts` imports it and its private `IMAGE_TYPES` is gone), `InlineImagePart`, `inlineImageHref(messageId, attachmentId)` (built in one place so the route and the read path cannot drift), and the pure `matchInlinePart(cid, parts)` - exact Content-ID first, brackets stripped and case-folded, then a **filename fallback** for every attachment recorded before this migration, and only when exactly one candidate matches, because a guess between two is worse than a missing picture. A part whose recorded type is not a picture is never returned. **`lib/html.ts` gains** `INLINE_SRC_ATTR` (`data-uin-cid`), `hasInlineImages`, `inlineImageCids` and `rewriteInlineImages(html, hrefFor)` - unresolved names lose the `src` entirely and park the name, so the tag renders its alt text rather than a broken picture; idempotent by construction. **READ PATH, not write path:** `getMessageHtml` in `lib/db.ts` now runs `withInlineImages` after `readableHtml`, for the same reason `readableHtml` is there - `lib/compose.ts` quotes the STORED markup under a reply, and a quote pointing at this site's own routes is a reply that sends the recipient to a login page. The database is only asked about a message that references a part at all. **NEW db functions:** `inlineImagePartsForMessage` and `getMessageAccess` (the access columns without the body - the inline route would otherwise pull a newsletter out of the database once per picture in it). `insertAttachment` gains `contentId`; `AttachmentRow`/`mapAttachment` gain `contentId`; `lib/sync.ts` passes `attachment.cid || attachment.contentId || null` (mailparser hands back `cid` with the brackets already off). **NEW TESTS:** 11 cases added to `lib/html.test.ts`, NEW `lib/inline-images.test.ts` (13), and NEW `lib/inline-images.live.test.ts` (6, gated on `RUN_INBOX_INLINE_GUARDS=1` + `OVH_*`; a skip is a FAIL) - because the migration, the INSERT and the new SELECTs are raw SQL and no typecheck, lint or build ever executes one. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, `vitest run modules/unified-inbox` **1089 passed / 229 skipped**, backup schema-coverage backstop 1 passed, and the live suite **6 passed** against a real throwaway `cactus_rt_uinline_*` database on the OVH VPS. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md` "Reading email safely" gains **Pictures that came inside the message are simply shown**; module README's pictures bullet says the same in one line.)

They were not - `SELECT send_at, send_state FROM uin_drafts` on the live site returned NULL for every row, and no outbound message matched the test. **Nothing had ever been scheduled at all.** A time picked off the alarm clock lives in `pendingSendAt` and reached the server ONLY through the Send later / Send later & snooze presses: `saveInBackground` (the quiet save when a click takes the screen elsewhere) called `draftPayload()` with no wall clock, and the close dialog called `save()` with none, so picking "Tomorrow morning" and then clicking onto the next conversation produced an ordinary draft with no time and no complaint. Chris's call: it gets set going. **`Composer.tsx`**: `pendingWallClock` moved up beside the state it is derived from (it is now read long before the button strip); `saveInBackground` builds its payload WITH the time and, on a refusal, posts the same writing once more WITHOUT it - a departure the server will not take must never cost somebody the words; `closeIsAQuestion` counts a pending time, and the dialog becomes **Set it going? / Save it and set it going** naming the time in words. **`ComposeView.tsx`**: same three - `askToLeave`, the cross's own handler and the `beforeunload` guard all count a pending time, and the leave dialog's confirm carries `toWallClock(pendingSendAt)` + `pendingFollowUp`. Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**A dropped file was attached twice, and the file dialog lists what is on the message** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. Follows straight on from the entry below. No schema change, no route change, no permission change, no core change, no new file. **THE DEFECT, and it is a class of defect rather than one bug: a portal is a portal in the DOM ONLY.** React sends an event up the tree it RENDERED, not the DOM tree, so a file dropped on the `createPortal`'d attachment dialog bubbled on to `Composer`/`ComposeView`, which hang the SAME `drop.dropProps` on their own box - two `onDrop` calls, two uploads, two chips, one drop. Reported from real use. **Choose files never did it**: it calls `drop.addFiles` directly and there is nothing to bubble, which is the tell that pointed at the event path rather than at the queue. **FIX in `AttachmentPicker.tsx`**: a `useMemo`'d `dropProps` wrapping all four of the hook's handlers with `event.stopPropagation()` before delegating, spread on the card in place of `drop.dropProps`. Stopped rather than untangled - the dialog is drawn over the composer, so what lands on it is the dialog's. The hook, the queue and the uploads route are untouched. **Worth remembering for `ProductPicker` and `ConfirmDialog`**, which are portalled out of the same composers: any handler a portal's React parent also carries will fire twice. **The dialog now LISTS what is on the message** under whichever half is showing (the drop box, or the search results): a `.uin-attach-list` of one `<li>` per file - name (ellipsised, full name on `title`), `describeFile` size, and a cross - under a `.uin-attach-heading` reading "On this message". New required prop `onRemove: (key: string) => void`, fed by both composers with the same filter they give `AttachmentChips`. The `countAttached()` helper and `.uin-modal-foot-note` are GONE: the foot is the Done button and nothing else. "3 files on this message" beside Done was the wrong end of the dialog and the wrong amount of detail for somebody who has just attached the wrong file. The chips row above the button strip stays as it is. **New CSS in `styles.tsx`** (tokens only): `.uin-attach-heading`, `.uin-attach-list`, `.uin-attach-list li`, `-name`, `-size`, `-x` (padded past 24px each way; hover and focus-visible both). **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint .` leaves ONE warning, `Composer.tsx(548,6) react-hooks/exhaustive-deps: missing dependency 'needsSomebody'` - **another session's in-flight channel-reply work in this same file, not this change**; my four files lint clean on their own. `vitest run` on `drafts`/`send`: 79 pass 0 fail. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md` **Attachments**: a paragraph saying what is on the message is listed underneath, a file to a line, with a cross to take one back off without closing the box.)

Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**The bulk bar only offers what would change something, and can bin a pile of junk** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. No schema change, no migration, no new route, no permission change, no core change, no new dependency, no new file. **TWO FILES:** `modules/unified-inbox/components/admin/inbox/ThreadListView.tsx` and `modules/unified-inbox/components/admin/InboxPanel.tsx`. **(1) DEAD BUTTONS.** The four bulk buttons were drawn unconditionally, so picking six conversations that were all open still offered **Open again**, and six already read still offered **Mark as read** - a press that fires six PATCHes to write down what was already written down, and a bar that reads as though there were work left. Each is now gated on a new `offer` memo over `pickedRows` (`rows` narrowed by `pickedSet`): `done` on `status !== 'done'`, `read` on `unread`, `unread` on `!unread`, `open` on `status !== 'open'`, each with `.some()` rather than `.every()` - five read and one unread is exactly when **Mark as read** is wanted. Read and unread are opposites, so the bar can never empty itself. **(2) NEW BULK ACTION: Mark as spam.** POSTs `{spam:true}` to `/api/m/unified-inbox/threads/[id]/spam` once per picked row (no new route - the single-conversation one already checks `canOpenThread` and works out whose bin via `spamOwnerFor`), then, only if at least one moved, opens a **`ConfirmDialog`** asking whether to block those senders too - one POST per distinct address to `/api/m/unified-inbox/blocked-senders`. Hidden in the Spam folder (`!spam`). The move happens FIRST and does not wait for an answer, same as `SpamButton`: Escape still leaves the junk moved. **NEW PROP `canBlock`** on `ThreadListView`, fed `canSendOut` (`unifiedinbox.reply`) from `InboxPanel.tsx` - marking junk is `view`, shutting the front door is `reply`. **(3) WHO GETS OFFERED FOR BLOCKING:** a `blockable` memo - distinct `normaliseAddress(row.participantAddress)` containing `@`, skipping discussions (`row.startedByUserId`) and every phone/SMS row. Whether an address is ALREADY blocked is deliberately NOT asked: `listBlockedSenders` is `manage`-grade reading that names colleagues, and shipping it to everyone with `view` to save a line of dialog copy is not the trade. `blockSender` is `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, so re-blocking is a no-op and the dialog says so. **(4) REFACTOR:** `applyToPicked` split into `runOnPicked(send)` - the shared `Promise.allSettled` + failure count + `clearPicked` + `router.refresh()` machinery, now RETURNING whether any succeeded - and a thin `applyToPicked(body)` over it. Addresses are captured BEFORE the run because the run clears the picking, and held in a `blocking: string[]` state that doubles as the dialog's `open`. **COPY TRAP:** the dialog does NOT name whose spam folder, unlike `SpamButton` - a pile picked off one list can span inboxes with different owners, and "moved to your spam" is a plain untruth over a colleague's own post; it states the rule instead. **WIKI:** `wiki/Unified-Inbox.md` "Working through several at once" rewritten - it still described the tick boxes that were removed when click/shift-click/cmd-click picking landed. `tsc --noEmit` and `eslint .` clean.)

Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**Filing a module's sent email is now its own switch, separate from the address it sends as** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. **ONE NEW MODULE MIGRATION**, no core change at all, no new route, no permission change, no new dependency. **ONE NEW FILE:** `modules/unified-inbox/lib/module-mail.live.test.ts`. **NEW SCRIPT:** `npm run test:inbox-module-mail` (`RUN_INBOX_MODULE_MAIL=1`, throwaway OVH database, a skip is a fail). **THE COMPLAINT:** shop order emails (shipped, confirmed) never appear in the inbox they were sent from. Nothing for `shop`, so nothing filed. `modules/shop/lib/email.ts:49` has always passed `moduleName: 'shop'`, so no shop change was needed then or now. **THE SPLIT:** `migrations/046_module_copy_inbox.sql` adds `uin_module_senders.copy_inbox_id TEXT` (nullable), drops `NOT NULL` from `inbox_id`, and `UPDATE`s `copy_inbox_id = inbox_id` so every install already filing carries on filing to the same place. Both FKs go **CASCADE -> SET NULL**: with two answers in one row, deleting an inbox must not delete where a module files. A row answering neither question is swept by `pruneEmpty` on the next write. **LOOKUPS:** `lib/module-senders.ts` gains `getModuleMailSettings`, `getModuleCopyInboxId`, `setModuleCopyInbox`; `setModuleSender` no longer DELETEs on null (it writes NULL, then prunes); `modulesForInbox` now matches EITHER column, since a module whose conversations are the filed copies belongs to that inbox most of all. `outbound-record.ts` reads `getModuleCopyInboxId`; `outbound-identity.ts` untouched. **API:** `app/api/admin/module-senders/route.ts` GET adds `copyInboxId`; PUT takes `inboxId` and/or `copyInboxId`, each nullable AND optional - **absent means do not touch**, which is what lets the panel save one dropdown without resending the other. **UI:** `ModuleSenderPanel` heading `Which inbox this comes from` -> `Where this email goes`, now two selects: **Send [what] from** and **Keep a copy of [what] in**. Both hosted panels (`ShopSenderPanel`, `PurchaseOrdersSenderPanel`) unchanged in wiring. **GATES RUN:** `npm run test:inbox-module-mail` 8/8 PASS against a real throwaway OVH database (the migration DDL, the upgrade UPDATE, independence of the two writes, prune, SET NULL, and the union in `modulesForInbox` all executed); `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` PASS; `npm run typecheck` clean for these files, `eslint` clean. Wiki: `Unified-Inbox.md`, `Purchase-Orders.md`, `Shop.md`, `Authoring-a-module.md`. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's.)

Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**A followed link now counts, on a reply and on a campaign** - `unified-inbox` click tracking, NOT yet released. **SCHEMA:** new module migration `modules/unified-inbox/migrations/047_click_tracking.sql` - `uin_messages.clicked_at` / `last_click_at` / `click_count`, `uin_campaign_sends.clicked_at`, and the delivery-event unique index SPLIT IN TWO: `uin_delivery_events_occurrence_key` is now PARTIAL (`WHERE kind <> 'clicked'`) and `uin_delivery_events_click_key` dedupes a click on (message, moment, link) instead - several links inside one second are several clicks, which is what an office mail scanner does to every address in an arriving message. `recordDeliveryEvent` therefore has TWO inserts, each naming its index's own WHERE clause so Postgres will infer the partial index. **BREVO:** `click` added to `BREVO_SUBSCRIBED_EVENTS`, so the registered webhook asks for it - it only ever arrives when link tracking is on in the Brevo account itself. `normaliseBrevoEvent` files `click`/`clicks` as kind `clicked` with the address in `detail` (`link`, `URL` or `url`; http/https only, 500 chars). A click sets `delivered_at` and deliberately does NOT touch the open columns. **ONE NEW FILE:** `modules/unified-inbox/lib/click-tracking.live.test.ts` (6 tests, real throwaway Postgres) + root script `npm run test:inbox-clicks` (`RUN_INBOX_CLICKS=1`) - the ON CONFLICT inference is raw SQL and no other gate executes it. **UI:** a *Followed a link* tag beside the open in `ThreadPane`, and the Sent-replies settings panel says what the Brevo-side switch is. **RE-REGISTRATION:** the subscribed-event list only ever reached Brevo from the settings PATCH, so an existing install would never have asked for `click` - `app/api/cron/housekeeping/route.ts` now calls `reconcileBrevoWebhooks(true)` nightly when `trackOpens` is on, which also repairs a webhook stranded by a restore.)
Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**Google Shopping links each variation at the address the sitemap publishes** - `shop-variations` v0.1.172 + `google-shopping-for-shop` v0.1.11 + core v0.5.1544 and v0.5.1545, all RELEASED. No schema change, no migration, no new route, no permission change, no new dependency. **ONE NEW FILE:** `modules/shop-variations/lib/variation-canonical-query.test.ts` (9 tests). **THE DEFECT:** `lib/feed-data.ts:296` linked every feed item at the variation's own hidden CHILD-product slug (`/italia-round-coffee-table-...-walnut-black`). That address renders the PARENT listing with the combination pre-selected, and `modules/shop/app/public/shop/products/[slug]/page.tsx:136` correctly declares the parent canonical for it - so Google followed a few thousand feed links, read a few thousand canonicals pointing elsewhere, and filed the lot under **"Alternative page with proper canonical tag"** instead of indexing them. Deskwell had 1,096, growing 7 -> 582 -> 1,096 over 18-29 Aug as Merchant Center worked through the feed. **Verified NOT a sitemap problem:** 0 of the 999 exported URLs appear in the live `sitemap.xml` (19,796 entries, 19,066 of them variation query-string addresses, all confirmed self-canonical against the live site). **NEW EXPORT** `variationCanonicalQuery(options, optionValueIds)` in `modules/shop-variations/lib/url-selection.ts`, plus the structural type `VariationUrlOption` - the ONE spelling of a variation's published address, and the one place that decides a combination has none. Returns null on: two options slugifying to the same parameter name, an option left unanswered, a value belonging to no option on the product, and two values of a single option. **THREE CALLERS, previously three copies of the rules:** `lib/sitemap.ts` (`buildVariationEntries` now builds `VariationUrlOption[]` per product from its two SQL row shapes and calls it; the `ambiguousProducts`/`valueIndex` blocks are gone, all 9 existing tests unchanged and passing), `lib/canonical-query-provider.ts` (its final spelling block collapses to one call; the derivation above it - `withStrandedFilled` -> `withAutoSelected` -> `resolveVariant` - is untouched), and `google-shopping-for-shop/lib/feed-data.ts` (imports it; `link` is now `${productUrl(siteUrl, parent.slug, style)}?${query}`). **ONE BEHAVIOUR CHANGE, in the canonical tag's favour:** on a product whose options slugify alike (`Colour` / `Colour!`), the page used to declare a SHORTENED canonical naming only the option that claimed the key - an address `lib/sitemap.ts` deliberately never published. It now declines and the bare listing stands, which is what the sitemap always assumed. **FEED FALLBACK:** where `variationCanonicalQuery` returns null the item keeps its child-slug link - it renders the right page, and a landing page that works beats a tidy one that does not. **`id` IS UNTOUCHED** (still `childProductId`), so Merchant Center matches the same listings and nothing is retired and re-created. **RELEASE ORDER WAS CORE-BETWEEN-MODULES, not modules-then-core:** the build gate's `scripts/ci/compose-module-registry.mjs` adds a candidate's `requiresModules` siblings **at the version core's `modules.json` pins**, so tagging google-shopping-for-shop before core pinned shop-variations 0.1.172 would have built it against a module with no `variationCanonicalQuery` and gone red on a public tag. Sequence: sv v0.1.172 -> core v0.5.1544 (pin only) -> gsf v0.1.11 -> core v0.5.1545 (pin + wiki). `google-shopping-for-shop/cactus.module.json` `requiresModules` shop-variations `minVersion` 0.1.146 -> 0.1.172. **GATES:** both module build gates completed:success; `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean whole repo, `npm test` 441 files / 5,964 tests passed 0 failed. No SQL, schema or backup code touched, so the round-trip and live-database gates were not demanded by these files. `wiki/Google-Shopping.md` gains "Why the feed's links changed in 0.1.11"; `wiki/Shop-variations.md` notes the feed now shares the sitemap's address. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's; the canonical tags above were read with `curl` against the live site.)

Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**Post filed into a watched folder by hand now surfaces in the list** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. **ONE NEW MODULE MIGRATION**, no core schema change, no new route, no permission change, no new dependency. **ONE NEW FILE:** `modules/unified-inbox/lib/arrival-order.live.test.ts`. **NEW SCRIPT:** `npm run test:inbox-arrival` (`RUN_INBOX_ARRIVAL=1`, throwaway OVH database, a skip is a fail). **THE DEFECT:** the conversation list is ordered on `uin_threads.last_message_at`, and `touchThread` only ever moves that FORWARD (`GREATEST(last_message_at, sentAt)`), which is what stops an out-of-order reply rewriting a conversation's history. A message dated BEHIND the conversation's newest one therefore lands correctly and moves nothing: same position, same preview, no visible change. That is every email somebody files into a watched folder after the fact. Seen live 2026-09-07 - a supplier reply dated 07:42 moved into `Deskwell/Purchasing` at 13:52, collected on the very next refresh, filed on thread `09f8117b` whose newest message was an outbound at 07:50, and invisible. **THE COLUMN:** `migrations/045_thread_arrival.sql` adds `uin_threads.last_arrived_at TIMESTAMP(3)` (nullable, NOT backfilled) plus `uin_threads_arrival_idx ON (GREATEST(last_message_at, last_arrived_at) DESC NULLS LAST, last_message_at DESC NULLS LAST, id DESC)` - written that way round so a backward scan of the one index also serves the oldest-first list. **WRITTEN ONLY WHEN THE MESSAGE DOES NOT MOVE THE THREAD FORWARD:** `touchThread` takes a new required `arrivedNow: boolean` and sets `last_arrived_at = now()` only when `arrivedNow AND last_message_at > sentAt`. Every other conversation keeps NULL for ever, `GREATEST` ignores NULLs, so their order is byte-for-byte what it was. **`arrivedNow` IS THE FORWARD PASS ONLY** - new `SyncPass = 'forward' | 'backfill'` threaded through `processBatch` -> `fileMessage` -> `writeSide` in `lib/sync.ts`; on the backfill every message is behind by definition and stamping them would float an entire mailbox's history to the top. **ORDER CONSTANTS:** `THREAD_LIST_ORDER` and `THREAD_LIST_ORDER_OLDEST` in `lib/db.ts` now lead with the `GREATEST(...)` and keep `last_message_at` as the second key, so two conversations that arrived in the same sweep still fall back to the date on the mail. `createThread` untouched - a NEW conversation from an old filed-in email still sorts on its mail date, which is a remaining gap, not a fix. **GATES RUN:** `npm run test:inbox-arrival` 5/5 PASS and `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` 4/4 PASS, both against real throwaway OVH databases; `tsc --noEmit`, `eslint`, `vitest run modules/unified-inbox` 1062 PASS.)
Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**Blocked post lands in the Spam folder, and the folder can say who is blocked** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. **ONE NEW MODULE MIGRATION**, no core change, no new route, no permission change, no new dependency. **ONE NEW FILE:** `components/admin/inbox/BlockedAddresses.tsx`. **WHAT CHANGED IN BEHAVIOUR:** mail from an address on `uin_blocked_senders` used to be recognised in `lib/sync.ts`, have its location recorded and be left on the mail server uncollected. It is now collected and filed like any other message, then stamped: out of every list, in the Spam folder, `status = 'done'`, `unread = true`. The reason is that "did they ever actually write?" previously had no answer anywhere on the site. **THE STAMP IS A COLUMN, NOT A ROW.** `migrations/044_blocked_to_spam.sql` adds `uin_threads.blocked_at TIMESTAMP(3)` (nullable) plus a partial index `uin_threads_blocked_idx ... WHERE "blocked_at" IS NOT NULL`. `uin_thread_spam` is one PERSON'S opinion of one conversation and could not carry this: a block is site-wide, nobody presses anything, and the colleague who blocked the address may have left. So both junk clauses in `lib/db.ts` learned to read a second thing - `spamMatch(viewer)` and `spamFolderMatch(owner)` are each now `(t."blocked_at" IS NOT NULL OR EXISTS (...))`, which hides refused post from every list for everybody and lists it in every REAL bin. **The `owner === null` branch of `filterClauses` is deliberately untouched** - a bin belonging to nobody (a shared address, or an id the reader may not open) still yields `false` outright, because ORing the stamp in there is how "nothing at all" becomes "nothing except the interesting part". **NEW `lib/db.ts` `setThreadBlocked(threadId, blocked)`** - on: `blocked_at = COALESCE(blocked_at, now())` (so a nuisance who writes six times has one date), `status = 'done'`, `snooze_until = NULL`, `unread = true`. Off: stamp cleared, status back to `'open'`, guarded `WHERE "blocked_at" IS NOT NULL`. `createThread` gains `blocked?: boolean` and now writes `status`/`blocked_at` explicitly. **`lib/spam.ts` `threadIsSpamFor` now queries `uin_threads`**, ORing the stamp with the person's row - without it the header on a refused conversation offered "Junk" while the reader was standing inside the Spam folder, i.e. no way out. **THE ONLY WAY OUT** is `POST /api/m/unified-inbox/threads/[id]/spam` with `spam: false`, which now also calls `setThreadBlocked(id, false)`. Still `unifiedinbox.view`: the line this module draws is about what happens FROM NOW ON, and rescuing one conversation the reader can already open is not that. The SENDER stays blocked. **`lib/sync.ts`:** `shouldRefuseSender` renamed `shouldJunkSender` (same logic, honest name; `lib/blocked-senders.test.ts` renamed with it), the early-return refusal branch replaced by `const junk = ...` threaded through `writeSide`. Gated off for junk: `reopenOnReply` (it would undo the stamp written two lines above and race per message), `holdScheduledDraftsFor` (a blocked sender must not stand down a quote), `ownPostAssignee` (refused post goes on nobody's desk) and `queueMessageWebhooks` at BOTH call sites (internal-pair and single). `FolderOutcome.discarded` no longer counts blocked mail - it lands in `stored`. **NEW UI:** a **Blocked addresses** button in the head of the Spam folder's list, between the search box and the filters. `Filters.tsx` gains `blockedAddresses: { canUnblock: boolean } | null`; `InboxPanel.tsx` passes `params.spamOnly && canManage ? { canUnblock: canSendOut } : null` - reading the list is `unifiedinbox.manage` (the grant the existing GET takes), lifting a block is `unifiedinbox.reply` (what the existing POST takes), so a manager without reply sees the list and no buttons. The dialog is mounted only while open, fetches the SAME `/api/m/unified-inbox/blocked-senders` the settings page uses, lists newest-blocked first with **Let them through**, and `router.refresh()`es once on close only if something changed. NEW icon `BlockedAddressesIcon` (struck-through @) rather than reusing `SpamIcon`/`BlockIcon` - the Spam folder draws the junk sign on every row, and a second ring-with-a-line in the toolbar over it is two buttons in one drawing. NEW styles `.uin-blocked-list/-row/-main/-address/-sub`. **Copy corrected everywhere it said "not collected":** `lib/blocked-senders.ts` header, `app/api/blocked-senders/route.ts`, `components/admin/settings/BlockedSendersSection.tsx` hint, `SpamButton.tsx`'s confirm dialog, and a dated NOTE appended to `migrations/041_spam.sql` rather than a rewrite of it. `wiki/Unified-Inbox.md` "Junk, and turning a sender away" rewritten with two new sub-sections. **NOT CHANGED, deliberate:** `lib/provider-sync.ts` still skips a blocked party's conversation outright - live chat, SMS, WhatsApp and forms are not email, the ask was about email, and dropping is stricter rather than weaker. A block therefore means two slightly different things by channel. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, `vitest run` 5955 passed / 346 skipped, `RUN_INBOX_SPAM_GUARDS=1` live suite 15/15 against a real throwaway Postgres (5 new tests: bin-for-everybody + done + unread, nobody's bin stays empty, stamped once, "Not junk" rescues and leaves the sender blocked, counts agree with lists), and `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` PASS 4/4 for the new column. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's.)

Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**The conversation you are reading counts as one of the ones you picked** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. No schema change, no migration, no new route, no permission change, no core change, no new dependency, no new file. **THE DEFECT:** in `components/admin/inbox/ThreadListView.tsx`, opening a conversation and then ctrl/cmd-clicking a second one left `selected` holding ONE id, so the bulk bar read "1 selected" and the **Merge** button - drawn only on `canManage && picked.length > 1` - never appeared, on the exact pair somebody had lined up to merge. Two things had made that look like a bug in Merge rather than in selection: the open row and a picked row are drawn on the SAME tint (`.uin-row[aria-current="true"]` and `.uin-list-item[data-selected="true"] > .uin-row` both take `--color-primary-subtle`), and `extendTo` ALREADY treats the open row as the anchor a shift-run is measured from - so shift-clicking worked and ctrl-clicking did not. **THE FIX:** `toggle` now seeds the selection with `openThreadId` when nothing on screen is picked yet, the open conversation is on this page (`onScreen`), and it is not the row being toggled - the mail-program reading, where the message in the reading pane is part of the selection. Consequences worth knowing: the seeded row is ticked and counted like any other, so **Mark as done / read / unread / Open again also reach the open conversation**, visibly ("2 selected", a tick where its avatar was) and reversibly (ctrl-click it again, or Clear). Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**Three defects on a conversation's context, and the catalogue reaches a discussion** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. No schema change, no migration, no new route, no permission change, no core change, no new dependency. **(1) THE ATTACH PICKER RETURNED NOTHING ON A DISCUSSION, EVER.** `lib/adapters/format.ts`'s `inList([])` turned an empty list into the sentinel `'\u0000-no-such-value'` on the reasoning that no real value could equal it. It cannot, because **Postgres will not accept a text parameter containing a NUL byte at all** - binding one fails the whole statement with `invalid byte sequence for encoding "UTF8": 0x00`, verified against the live database through `pg`. Every adapter's `suggest` puts the person's addresses in an `ORDER BY ... IN (...)` ranking clause, so a conversation with nobody outside it - a discussion between colleagues has no addresses - threw, `suggestRecords` swallowed the throw per adapter, and the picker drew 'Nothing here matches that' for orders, quotes AND purchase orders alike. Empty now renders as `IN (NULL)` (`Prisma.sql` with nothing bound), which is never true and which Postgres takes. Both shapes were run against the live `shp_orders`/`po_orders` before and after. New tests in `lib/adapters/format.test.ts` assert nothing this returns ever carries a NUL, plus `likeTerm`'s escaping. `lib/adapters/types.ts`: the `ContextQuery.emails` comment no longer says 'never empty in practice'. **NOTE: `NOT IN` on this would be a trap** - `x NOT IN (NULL)` is NULL, not true - and the helper says so. **(2) TAKING A PIECE OF CONTEXT OFF SILENTLY DID NOTHING.** `ContextRecords.tsx` dismisses its chevron menu on any `pointerdown` outside `wrap`/`menu`. `ConfirmDialog` renders through a **portal into `document.body`**, so it is outside both: pressing 'Take it off' closed the menu on pointerdown, `LinkActions` and the dialog unmounted with it, and the click never landed on the button that had been under the cursor. New `inOwnDialog(target)` (`target.closest('.uin-modal')`) guards both the pointerdown handler and the scroll/resize `dismiss`. **Any other menu that portals a dialog out of itself has the same shape of bug.** **(3) THE PICKER RANKED BY THE MATCHED PERSON, NOT BY THE CONVERSATION.** It built its `ContextQuery` from `thread.personId` alone, so a thread nobody was matched to ranked by nothing and a supplier writing from a shared address ranked by whoever the thread happened to be pinned to. NEW `lib/db.ts` `addressesOnThread(threadId, limit=200)` - `from_address` + `to_addresses` + `cc_addresses` off the thread's messages, newest first, deduped. NEW `lib/identity.ts` `buildThreadContextQuery(threadId, personId)` - those addresses put through the SAME gate mail goes through (`shouldBecomePerson`: our inboxes, our staff, our domains, `no-reply` and friends all dropped), unioned with the matched person's own query, with non-personal domains derived from the result. Null when there is genuinely nobody, which is what a discussion is. `app/api/m/unified-inbox/threads/[id]/links` GET uses it. Effect: orders from anybody on the conversation float to the top, newest first within that; purchase orders match on the SUPPLIER'S DOMAIN, so a thread with `sales@dynamicos.co.uk` on it opens on Dynamic's own POs. Proved on the live database - `mlangtip@netflix.com`'s order jumps from third to first. **(4) THE CATALOGUE NOW WORKS IN A NOTE, WHICH IS THE ONLY BOX A DISCUSSION HAS.** The tag button was gated `!noting`, and a discussion's composer arrives on note mode and stays there, so the one conversation whose whole purpose is 'look at this one' could not. `Composer.tsx`: the gate drops to `canAddProducts`, the title/aria say 'in this note' when noting, and the note POST now sends `products` (references only). `lib/validation.ts` `NoteBody` gains `products: z.array(ProductRefBody).max(20)`. `lib/notes.ts` `noteHtml(text, renderProduct?)` - the product SLOTS come out first (`replaceSlots`, markers of letters and digits so escaping cannot touch them), the words are escaped exactly as before, and each product's markup goes back where its slot was; called with one argument it behaves identically, which is what `app/api/discussions` and the existing tests still do. The notes route resolves the references SERVER-SIDE (`resolveProducts`) so a name or a price in a request cannot put words in the shop's mouth, writes `renderProductTable` into `body_html` and `renderProductText` into `body_text`, feeds the same words to `notifyMentions`, and `recordLink`s each product onto the conversation (guarded by `threadHasLink`) - exactly as `provider-send.ts` does when the same chair goes out in an email. **A note is drawn from `body_text`, not `body_html`** (see the comment in `ThreadPane`), so on screen the product reads as its name, options, price and link rather than as the table; the table is in `body_html` ready for the day notes are rendered as markup. **REMAINDER, deliberate:** `productsTravel()` in `ThreadPane` still keeps the catalogue off `sms` and `phone` threads outright, so a NOTE on one of those does not get it either, though a note is never billed by the character. Lifting that needs the channel test to become reply-only, which means a new prop through `ThreadPane` - left alone because **another session is rewriting `Composer.tsx`, `ThreadPane.tsx`, `lib/validation.ts`, `lib/db.ts`, `RichText.tsx` and `lib/html.ts` in this same worktree right now** (43 modified files); the 'notes are drawn as markup' comment in `Composer.tsx` is theirs and unreleased. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, `vitest run` 5955 passed / 0 failed / 346 skipped. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's.)

That fallback also deletes `carried.status`, so the dead word does not ride on every link. `showUnassigned = showStatusTabs && sharedInbox`; the mentions list gets `askStatus` (the queue coerced to 'open') because an ask belongs to one named person by definition and `MentionStatusFilter` has no such value. **`FilterMenu.tsx`** routes all three assignee choices through a new `assignTo()` which sets `status: 'open'` when the list is the queue - picking a name on top of it would AND two contradictory things and show an empty list. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint modules/unified-inbox` clean, `vitest run modules/unified-inbox` **1058 passed / 0 failed / 205 skipped** with 3 new cases in `lib/list.test.ts`. Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**Attaching a file is a dialog now, and it takes as many as you like** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. No schema change, no migration, no new route, no permission change, no core change, no new dependency, no new file. **THE COMPLAINT:** the paperclip unfolded a panel *inside* the composer holding a search box over the media library and nothing else, so "attach a file" quietly meant "attach a file that is already in the library". The ordinary errand - the quote on somebody's desktop - had no button at all: it could be dragged onto the message and that was the whole of it, which is a gesture nobody discovers by looking. **`components/admin/inbox/AttachmentPicker.tsx` rewritten** (helpers below the component - `plainReason`, `toHtml`, `formatSize`, `kindOf`, `describeFile`, `AttachmentChips`, the `Attachment` type - all untouched and still exported from here). It is now `createPortal`'d onto `document.body` as a `uin-modal` + `uin-modal-card uin-modal-card-attach uin-droppable`, same as `ProductPicker`, because the composer sits in a pane that scrolls its own contents. Head, body, foot: search box, then EITHER the big drop box or the results, then the progress/errors notice; foot carries the count and **Done**. **Search decides which half shows** - non-empty query = results, empty = the big box back. New props: `drop: AttachmentDrop` and `attached: Attachment[]`. **The upload queue is the COMPOSER's, handed down** rather than started in the dialog: `useAttachmentDrop` aborts everything in flight on unmount, so a hook owned by the dialog would kill every upload the moment somebody pressed Done. `drop.dropProps` go on the card (the whole card, not the rectangle) and the overlay is drawn over it. A library row already on the message renders `disabled` reading "Already on this message". Escape still stands down for `[data-uin-confirm]`; keyboard starts in the search box and is put back where it came from. The old `term === null` state is gone - `ask()` sets "looking" and clears in the handler, because `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` is an ERROR in this tree and the effect may only fetch. **NEW `drop.addFiles(files, refusals?)` in `useAttachmentDrop.tsx`**: the one way in for both gestures. `gather()` now returns raw files plus folder refusals only; `refuseDroppedFile` and the count check moved into `addFiles`, which `onDrop` calls with what it gathered synchronously. The hidden `<input type="file" multiple>` behind **Choose files** goes through the same path, so drop and chooser refuse identically. **Chips moved out of the button strip** onto their own `uin-composer-row uin-attachment-row` above it, in BOTH composers (`Composer.tsx`, `ComposeView.tsx`) - three files used to push Send onto a second line. The row is not rendered at all when there is nothing on the message. **Neither picker closes after a pick any more** (the file dialog now matches the catalogue). **Counts raised**: `MAX_DROPPED_FILES` 20 -> 100 in `lib/uploads.ts`, and `attachments` in `lib/validation.ts` `.max(20)` -> `.max(100)` on BOTH `SendBody` and `DraftBody` so a draft can never hold more than the message it becomes. `products` stays at 20. The real ceiling is unchanged and is a WEIGHT, not a count: `checkAttachmentBudget` in `lib/compose.ts` still refuses the lot in English before a connection is opened, and the per-file 4MB `MAX_DROPPED_BYTES` is untouched. The route's own `MAX_DROPPED_FILES` check is per REQUEST and the browser posts one file per request, so it never binds. **New CSS in `styles.tsx`** (tokens only, no hex): `.uin-modal-card-attach`, `.uin-attach-find`, `.uin-attach-results`, `.uin-dropzone` + `-icon`/`-title`/`-sub`/`-note`, `.uin-modal-foot-note`, `.uin-attachment-row`. **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint .` clean across the whole tree, `vitest run` on `drafts`/`send`/`channel-reply`/`outbound-record` 98 pass 0 fail. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md` **Attachments** section rewritten: what the dialog looks like, that it stays open, that the search greys out what is already on, that the tags now sit on their own line, and a paragraph saying there is no sensible limit on how many because the nine megabytes is the limit you actually meet.)

Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**WhatsApp does have bold, so it is offered - and it now arrives** - `unified-inbox` + `twilio` + one CORE type addition, uncommitted working tree. Additive and optional: every existing provider is unchanged and reads as a channel that carries plain words. The shape cannot express a style whose opening and closing markers differ, deliberately - no chat channel writes one, and a shape that could would need a parser rather than a wrapper. **THE POINT OF PUTTING IT ON THE PROVIDER:** the sender never learns another module's wire format. unified-inbox does not know what an asterisk means to WhatsApp; it is handed markers as data. **`twilio/lib/whatsapp-conversation-provider.ts`** declares `textStyles: {bold: '*', italic: '_', strikethrough: '~'}`. Monospace is left OFF on purpose though WhatsApp has it (```` ```x``` ````): no writing box here can produce it, and a declared style is a button offered, so claiming it would draw a button that does nothing. Colour, links-with-wording and lists are not WhatsApp features at all. **NEW `unified-inbox/lib/text-styles.ts`** (pure): `applyTextStyles(html, styles)` turns `<strong>/<b>`, `<em>/<i>`, `<s>/<strike>/<del>` and `<code>/<tt>` into the declared markers, innermost first via a same-tag-excluding regex so `<b>a <b>c</b> d</b>` is not mangled, hoisting whitespace OUTSIDE the marker (WhatsApp reads `* hello *` as a literal asterisk), dropping markers round nothing, and refusing to wrap the same marker twice. `offeredStyles(styles)` names what to draw buttons for. Plus `lib/text-styles.test.ts` (13 tests). **`lib/provider-send.ts`**: `replyWords(html, refs, styles?)` now flattens through `htmlToText(applyTextStyles(html, styles))` - markers BEFORE the tags come off, since the emphasis lives in the tags - and the same `flatten` is handed to `flattenWithProducts`, so nothing in `products/slots.ts` changed. **`sendProviderReply` input changed: `text: string` -> `body: {html} | {text}`.** The rendering moved inside because rendering needs the channel's markers and this is the function that already resolves the channel; a caller doing it would resolve the provider twice. An empty body is still refused BEFORE any query (`htmlHasWriting` on the markup, `.trim()` on the words) and again after rendering. Both call sites updated - `app/api/send/route.ts` and `lib/scheduled-send.ts` - and `replyWords` is still exported and still tested directly. **`lib/provider-registry.ts`**: `ProviderChannel` gains `textStyles`, read off `provider.capabilities?.textStyles ?? null`. **`lib/channel-reply.ts`**: `ReplyStyle.richText` (the whole six-button email strip) is joined by `styles: readonly TextStyleName[]`; `replyStyleFor(thread, channelStyles)` filters the declaration through a fixed `STYLE_NAMES` order so two channels declaring the same three draw them the same way round. **`components/admin/inbox/RichText.tsx`**: `RichTextTools` gains an optional `styles` prop and a `TextStyleName` export. Absent = the six as before. No SQL, no migration, no column: backup round-trip not triggered. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. **RELEASE ORDER when this ships: core first** (both modules compile against the new capability field), then `twilio`, then `unified-inbox`; `twilio`'s `requiresCoreVersion` needs the new core version. Module README and `wiki/Unified-Inbox.md` updated.)

Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**Scheduled is a folder of its own, and a waiting message can still be changed** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. No schema change, no migration, no new route, no permission change, no core change, no new dependency. **THE TWO DEFECTS, both reported from real use.** (1) A message set to go out sat under **Drafts** wearing a tag, so the Drafts count read as work outstanding when half of it was work already decided. (2) Worse: once `sendState` was `scheduled` the composer hid the PendingSend row, the **Send & snooze** dropdown and the primary button - so on a waiting message, picking a new time off the alarm clock set `pendingSendAt` and then had NOTHING to commit it (a silent no-op), there was no way to save an edit and keep the time, and "send it now after all" was no answer at all. **NEW SPLIT IN `lib/db.ts`:** two SQL fragments, `DRAFT_WAITING` (`send_state = 'scheduled' OR = 'sending'`) and `DRAFT_NOT_WAITING` (`IS NULL OR = 'failed'`), written out rather than one negated - `send_state` is NULL on every ordinary draft and `NOT (send_state IN (...))` is NULL, which would have emptied Drafts on every install while typechecking perfectly. `listDrafts`, `countDrafts` and `countDraftsByInbox` gain `AND DRAFT_NOT_WAITING`; NEW `listScheduledDrafts` (ordered `send_at ASC`, not `updated_at DESC` - the question is what goes next) and `countScheduledDrafts`. A **failed** one stays under Drafts on purpose: it is not going anywhere on its own any more and wants somebody. **NEW `standDownScheduledDraft(id, userId)` -> `'ready' | 'in-flight'`** - one UPDATE clearing the four schedule columns `WHERE send_state IS DISTINCT FROM 'sending'`, then a SELECT to tell "no such draft of theirs" from "a run has it". **`app/api/send/route.ts` calls it before both send paths** (`timerIsOff`, after the access checks so a refused send does not silently un-schedule): sending by hand a message the queue is posting this second would otherwise go out TWICE, because the composer's idempotency key is its own and the queue's is `scheduled-<draft id>` - two keys, two messages. 409 with a plain sentence. **`lib/list.ts`**: `scheduledOnly` param, `inbox=scheduled` (no scoped `scheduled:<id>` form - one folder, whichever address it leaves from), added to the inboxId exclusion list and to `NOT_A_SCOPE`. **`NavRail`**: new `showScheduled` / `scheduledCount` props, entry beside Drafts using the existing `AlarmIcon`, `Count word="waiting"`. **`InboxPanel`**: `countScheduledDrafts` joins the existing `Promise.all`, the list is fetched only on that tab, `listing` includes it so the thread queries are skipped, plus `currentTab` / `viewTitle` / `headTotal` / `showScheduled={scheduledCount > 0 || currentTab === 'scheduled'}`. **`DraftListView` draws both folders** - new optional `scheduled` prop, which changes only the empty state. **COMPOSERS (`Composer.tsx` and `ComposeView.tsx`, the same four changes in both):** the `!waiting` gates come off `PendingSend`, the snooze dropdown and the primary button; `PendingSend` gains `replacing` ("Move it to Friday at 09:00" / "Keep the time it has"); a NEW **Save changes** chip appears only while `waiting && !pending && hasUnsaved` and calls `save()` with `sendAt` undefined, which the drafts route reads as "leave whatever time is on it"; and ComposeView's leave dialog no longer says "Keep this as a draft? Nothing here has been sent" over a message that is leaving on Monday. `ScheduleNotice` says what can still be done. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean whole repo, `npx vitest run` **5923 passed / 0 failed** (1 new parse case). **`RUN_INBOX_SENT_GUARDS=1 sent-list.live.test.ts` a real PASS, 28 tests, no skips** against a throwaway `cactus_rt_*` database on the OVH VPS (OVH creds exported out of the Deskwell workspace `.env` for the run, never written here) - 6 NEW cases, because raw SQL is executed by no standard gate: a timed message leaves Drafts and its per-address count, the scheduled list is soonest-first and author-scoped, a failed one is back under Drafts, the stand-down clears the timer and returns the row to Drafts, one mid-send answers `'in-flight'` and is left exactly as the run left it, and a colleague's is untouched. Backup gate not triggered: no column, no table, no sequence, nothing in `lib/backup/**`. Module README and `wiki/Unified-Inbox.md` say so. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's.)

Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**The search dialog and the pen's menu were trapped behind the columns** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. No schema, route, permission or CSS change - two components, `createPortal` in both. **THE DEFECT, and it is a class of defect rather than one bug:** `44bb5ad` made `.uin-rail-me` (the reader's name, the magnifier and the pen, at the head of the rail) `position: sticky; top: 0`. **A sticky box makes a stacking context**, and a `position: fixed` descendant cannot escape one however high its z-index: `.uin-modal` at `z-index: 10000` inside it no longer competes with the panes at all, it competes at `.uin-rail-me`'s own level (auto, so 0) inside `.uin-app`. `.uin-thread-head` is `position: sticky; z-index: 3` and `.uin-notebar` is 2, both of which then paint OVER it - so pressing the magnifier drew the whole search dialog underneath the middle and right columns. Same trap, same head, one button along: `ComposeMenu`'s `.uin-compose-menu` is `position: fixed` at window coordinates and was going under the same two. **The fix is the pattern this module already uses** for `ConfirmDialog`, `UndoToast`, `Composer`, `ProductPicker` and (from the same commit) `NewMailNotifier`: `createPortal(..., document.body)`, guarded `typeof document !== 'undefined'`, neither ever open on a first render so the two sides agree. `ComposeMenu` needed one more thing with it - its outside-press handler asked only `wrap.current.contains(target)`, and with the menu no longer inside `wrap` that closed the menu out from under the press on its own entries, so it now also asks `menu.current`. **Worth remembering when adding anything sticky:** every fixed panel already inside that box stops working, silently, and nothing typechecks or lints it. `.uin-find` (the search screen's own head) is a grid item rather than a fixed one and is unaffected. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint` clean on both files, `vitest run modules/unified-inbox` **1021 passed / 197 skipped**. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's.)

One grouped query rather than one `countDrafts` per colleague, because the rail is drawn on every list the hub renders. Author-scoped means the number under Sam's name can never be Sam's writing. **No new routing needed:** `'drafts'` was already in `SCOPED_FOLDERS` in `lib/list.ts`, the panel already narrowed `listDrafts(user.id, folderIds)`, and `currentFolderInbox` in NavRail already read `drafts:` - the rail simply never offered the link. `folderInbox` is still resolved against `visible` before anything is fetched (E17), so a typed id yields nothing rather than everything. **Head of the scoped list** now reads `Drafts \u00b7 <address>` - the ADDRESS, never the colleague's name: which address it sits on and whose writing it is are two different facts, and nothing else on that screen said it was narrowed. Spam keeps the opposite rule under Yours (always offered, see its own comment): junk lands there by a press, so a folder that appeared only once full would read as having eaten the message. **Gates:** `tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint` clean, `vitest run modules/unified-inbox` **1017 passed / 188 skipped / 0 failed**. **`RUN_INBOX_SENT_GUARDS=1 sent-list.live.test.ts` real PASS, 12 tests, no skips** against a throwaway `cactus_rt_uinsent_*` database on the OVH VPS (OVH creds exported out of the Deskwell workspace `.env` for the run, never written here) - one NEW case pins `countDraftsByInbox`: per-address numbers for the author only, a colleague's draft on a readable address left out, the unfiled one left out, and an empty record when the GROUP BY returns no rows. Backup gate not triggered: reads only, no column, no table, no sequence. Module README and `wiki/Unified-Inbox.md` (Drafts section plus the Team-inboxes folder table, where the old "there is no Drafts folder here" paragraph is now the opposite) say so. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's.)

Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**A Spam folder per person, junk that follows whose post it is, and a block list for the whole site** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. Both in `teardown` (`uin_thread_spam` before `uin_threads`; `teardown.test.ts` enforces the order). **WHOSE BIN IS NOT ALWAYS THE PRESSER'S.** `spamOwnerFor` (pure, in `lib/spam.ts`): a conversation in an INDIVIDUAL inbox belongs to its owner, so somebody covering Sam's post who bins something fills SAM's spam folder, not their own; a SHARED address has no owner, so it is the presser's own opinion. The conversation's own `inbox_id` decides, never the addresses a merge added - one home is an answer that can be explained. Resolved server-side in the route; the body carries one boolean and could never name a user id. **TWO SQL BUILDERS IN `lib/db.ts`, and they ask different questions.** `spamMatch(viewer)` hides a conversation marked by the reader OR by the owner of any individual inbox it sits in - covering somebody means seeing what they would see, so a bin that emptied for only one of them would leave the coverer working post its owner had dealt with. `spamFolderMatch(owner)` is one named person's bin and nothing else, because answering the folder with the wider rule would tip every colleague's junk into everybody's own. `ThreadListFilters` gains REQUIRED `viewerUserId` (it broke all five live-test call sites at compile time, which is the point) and optional `spamOnly` / `spamOwnerUserId` - where **`undefined` means "my own" and an explicit `null` means "nobody", yielding `false` rather than falling back**, so a folder scoped to a shared address never draws the reader's own junk under a colleague's name (E17). One clause in `filterClauses` beside the merge clause, so every list, count and status tally goes through it; **`unreadCounts` takes `viewerUserId` FIRST** and uses the same builder. **`lib/list.ts`**: `InboxParams.spamOnly`; `'spam'` added to `SCOPED_FOLDERS`, so `spam:<inbox id>` is the folder under a colleague's name - scoped by WHOSE BIN rather than by which address, which is why the panel refuses to find an owner for a shared one. **NEW MODULE ROUTES:** `POST /threads/[id]/spam` (`unifiedinbox.view` + `canOpenThread`; answers `{spam, ownerUserId}`) and `GET|POST /blocked-senders` (GET `unifiedinbox.manage`, POST `unifiedinbox.reply` - blocking changes what everybody receives). New Zod `ThreadSpamBody`, `BlockedSenderBody`. **ENFORCEMENT AT BOTH INGEST POINTS.** `lib/sync.ts`: `FolderContext.blockedSenders`, read once per account, checked in `fileMessage` AFTER `findOutboundByMessageId`/`matchRelayCopy` and AFTER `placeMessage` - a reply typed on a colleague's phone comes back out of Sent as OUTBOUND, and refusing it would stop their own writing reaching its conversation. `lib/provider-sync.ts`: one `blockedSenderSet()` per pass, `continue` before `upsertProviderThread` when `party.email` matches - an enquiry form is the obvious way round an email block. **UI:** new `SpamIcon`/`BlockIcon`; NEW `SpamButton.tsx`, first child of `ThreadActions`, left of the snooze clock. The move happens on the press and does NOT wait for an answer; the block question follows in a `ConfirmDialog` and Escape leaves the move done. `ownerName` threads through `ThreadPane.spamState` -> `ThreadActions` -> `SpamButton` so the button and the dialog say "Sam's spam folder" rather than "your spam folder" when that is the truth. `NavRail`: **Spam** last under Yours, plus a **Spam** folder under each colleague beside Sent and Mentioned (guarded on `ownerUserId`, like Mentioned). `ThreadListView` gains `spam`/`spamOwnerName` for its own empty state, first in the ternary so an empty bin is not reported as "nothing has been collected yet". `InboxPanel` reads the sender off the newest INBOUND message (`replyTo` beats `from`, E13), and names a colleague's bin in the list head for the same reason Mentioned does. **SETTINGS:** `blockedSenders` on the `/admin/settings` payload; NEW `BlockedSendersSection.tsx` under `CollectingPanel`, below its Save button. Blocking is only ever done from a conversation (the address is read off the message); lifting one is only ever done here. **Gates:** `tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint .` clean, `vitest run modules/unified-inbox` **1017 passed / 182 skipped** (new: `blocked-senders.test.ts` 7, `spam.test.ts` 6, 2 in `provider-sync.test.ts`, 4 in `list.test.ts`). **Backup round-trip: real PASS** against throwaway OVH databases, byte-identical restore. **NEW `lib/spam.live.test.ts` (`RUN_INBOX_SPAM_GUARDS=1`, 10 tests) - real PASS**, because none of the above SQL is executed by any standard gate and the hide clause is nested three deep. It pins all six claims: one person's opinion on a shared address, a colleague's post landing in THEIR bin, it leaving the coverer's lists too, a shared-address owner's opinion NOT leaking to colleagues, a nobody-scoped folder drawing empty rather than the reader's own, tallies agreeing with lists, and the block list normalising on the way in. PRE-EXISTING and NOT touched: `lib/puck/module-layout-site-logo.test.tsx > shopInvoice renders it with the site's own logo` times out at 5s - nothing it depends on is modified in this tree. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's.)

Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**The browser nudges a colleague when post lands in their own address** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. No schema change, no new permission key, no core change, no new dependency and no service worker - these are ordinary in-page browser notifications, so nothing is delivered while the tab is shut. **NEW MODULE ROUTE `GET /api/m/unified-inbox/notifications`** (`app/api/notifications/route.ts`), `unifiedinbox.view`. Takes `?since=<ISO instant>` and answers `{now, arrivals[], total}`. `now` is the SERVER's clock, read BEFORE the query so nothing that lands mid-round is skipped for good; the browser keeps it verbatim and hands it back next time, so the two clocks never have to agree. No `since` means "just tell me the time" and is answered without touching the conversations. Scope: the reader's own inbox (`defaultInboxIdFor`, re-checked against `visibleInboxIds`) when they have one, otherwise every inbox they may read plus the non-hidden provider channels. Built on `listThreads`/`countThreads` with `unreadOnly: true`, `status: 'open'`, `after: since`, `perPage: MAX_ARRIVALS + 1` - the extra row is what lets an ordinary round skip the count query. E17 unchanged: inbox ids go INTO the query. **NEW `lib/notify.ts`** - `Arrival`, `ArrivalsReply`, `MAX_ARRIVALS` (5), `MAX_LOOKBACK_MS` (20 min), `parseSince`, `senderLabel`, `notificationCopy`, `storageKey`, `readPreference`, `writePreference`, `shouldPoll`. Plus `lib/notify.test.ts` (23 tests). `parseSince` refuses a future mark outright and clamps an ancient one to 20 minutes, so a tab left open over a weekend does not announce four days of post. **NEW `components/admin/inbox/NewMailNotifier.tsx`** ('use client') - the bell at the foot of the rail and the one-time offer under it. THE PREFERENCE IS IN `localStorage`, keyed `uin:notify:<userId>`, NOT the database: the permission itself is per browser, so a row saying "on" would describe nothing. Polls `/notifications` every 60s ONLY while the window is not focused, after a 15s settle, and the first round of each spell away is silent (it only fetches the mark). **`NavRail.tsx`**: new `NewMailNotifier` mount, new `bellShown` state, new `ownInboxName` local; the `uin-rail-foot` box is NO LONGER gated on `canCheckNow` (the Updated line, the notice and the refresh button are, individually) - a site whose only channels are a chat widget and a form had no bell at all. New `data-bare` attribute hides an empty foot. **`icons.tsx`**: new `BellIcon`, `BellOffIcon`. **`styles.tsx`**: `.uin-notify`, `.uin-notify-toggle`, `.uin-notify-offer`, `.uin-notify-offer-text`, `.uin-notify-offer-buttons`, `.uin-rail-foot[data-bare="1"]`; offer drops below the bell under 1200px, where the rail lies down.)
