Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Both writing boxes rebuilt: an editable To line, Bcc, a subject you can change, a pop-out, formatting, and the send buttons regrouped** - core + `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. **TWO NEW MODULE MIGRATIONS.** `migrations/033_bcc.sql`: `uin_messages.bcc_addresses` and `uin_drafts.bcc_addresses`, both `TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT ARRAY[]::TEXT[]`. `migrations/034_draft_body_format.sql`: `uin_drafts.body_format TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'text'` + `uin_drafts_body_format_check` (`'text'`/`'html'`). Both idempotent, no dollar quoting. **CORE.** `lib/email/index.ts`: `EmailPayload.bcc?: string[]`, threaded to Brevo (`bcc: [{email}]`) and to nodemailer (`bcc: 'a, b'`) - its own list, never folded into `cc`, which is the whole of what a blind copy is. `lib/sanitize-config.ts`: `b` and `i` added to `EMAIL_HTML_ALLOWED_TAGS` only (NOT to the richtext list) - a contentEditable box's bold is `<b>` in every browser, and stripping it silently unbolds what somebody plainly bolded. 3 new cases in `lib/email/send.test.ts`. **MODULE, BCC END TO END.** `SendBody.bcc` / `DraftBody.bcc` (Zod, max 50); `SendRequest.bcc` -> `prepareSend` (validated with to/cc, and **never derived from the conversation whatever the mode** - nobody was blind-copied by mail we can read) -> `insertOutboundMessage.bccAddresses` (optional, `?? []`) -> `SendableMessage.bcc` -> `deliver` -> core; `RawMessageInput.bcc` so the IMAP Sent copy is honest; `retrySend` reads `message.bccAddresses` back off the row; `scheduled-send.ts` passes `draft.bcc`. `Draft.bcc`, `DraftForComposer.bcc`, `DraftInput.bcc?`, `isWorthSaving` counts one. 4 new cases in `lib/send.test.ts`. **MODULE, RICH TEXT.** NEW `components/admin/inbox/RichText.tsx` - contentEditable + `document.execCommand`, deliberately no new dependency: bold, italic, seven colours, link, bullet list, numbered list, Ctrl/Cmd+B and +I. `styleWithCSS` false for bold/italic (`<b>`/`<i>`, what email is made of) and true for colour (`<span style="color:...">`); buttons `preventDefault` on **mousedown** or the selection they act on is gone before the click; the box is UNCONTROLLED and written into only when `value` differs, or the caret jumps to the front on every keystroke; paste is forced to `text/plain`. `Draft.bodyFormat` (`'text'|'html'`) says which of two things the body column holds - **a column, not a guess at the content**, or "a < b" renders as broken markup and a pasted line of HTML sends as live markup. Old drafts open through `plainTextToHtml` and save back as markup. NEW pure `draftBodyText()` + `htmlHasWriting()` in `lib/drafts.ts` (11 new cases) used by the Drafts list, the read-only view of a colleague's draft, the scheduled sender's provider-reply text and every "is there anything written" test. **The drafts route sanitises on the way IN** (`sanitizeEmailHtml`) - a draft is readable by whoever can read the address and is put back into another person's writing box, so cleaning it on the way out would leave the row a loaded gun for anything that ever read it without remembering to. **MODULE, THE TWO COMPOSERS.** `Composer.tsx`: To is a `RecipientField` seeded from the server's `replyTo`/`replyAllTo` and refilled as the mode chips change **until somebody edits it**; separate `forwardTo` bucket, because a forward must never inherit the customer's address. Cc / Bcc / Subject are links at the end of the To row, each opening its own `uin-field-row`; Subject opens on the server-computed default (NEW `replySubject`/`forwardSubject` props, worked out in `InboxPanel` from `newest?.subject ?? thread.subject` - the same fallback `prepareSend` uses). NEW pop-out: `createPortal` into `document.body` behind `poppedOut` state, so the component and everything typed in it survive the move; body scroll locked, Escape collapses. `ComposeView.tsx` gets the same bottom strip, the same Bcc line and `submit(snoozeUntil?)` which PATCHes the thread the send just created. **Send button strip, both boxes:** paperclip icon + alarm icon on the left, `.uin-composer-gap` (margin auto), then Throw away / Save as a draft / [Send later, Send later & snooze] / Send & snooze / **Send now**. Send & snooze and Send later & snooze open `SnoozePanel` with a `title` prop; there is deliberately NO Send later & snooze on the new-message box - a message that has not gone has not started a conversation, and the chase covers that case. NEW `SendLaterPanel.tsx` (alarm-clock menu, same words and same `snoozeOptions` as the conversation's own clock, plus Cancel the timer), NEW `PendingSend.tsx` (the picked-but-not-committed time + the follow-up chase, shared by both boxes), NEW `Calendar.tsx` (`TimeCalendar` + `landsOn`, lifted out of `SnoozePanel` so two menus share one month), NEW `ScheduleNotice.tsx` (the failed / held / scheduled sentences). **`SendLater.tsx` DELETED** - its picker is the alarm menu, its chase is `PendingSend`, its notices are `ScheduleNotice`; nothing it did was dropped. `minSendAt` removed from `Composer`/`ComposerSlot`/`ThreadPane`/`ComposeView`/`InboxPanel` with the datetime-local box that wanted it. NEW icons: `ExpandIcon`, `CollapseIcon`, `LinkIcon`, `ListIcon`, `NumberedListIcon`, `PaletteIcon`. `styles.tsx`: `.uin-richtext*`, `.uin-rt-*`, `.uin-field-links`, `.uin-field-pop`, `.uin-composer-actions`, `.uin-composer-gap`, `.uin-pending-send`, `.uin-mention-search`, `.uin-composer-aside`, `.uin-composer > .uin-fields`; `.uin-sendlater*` removed. **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint .` clean whole repo, `vitest run` 5668 passed / 0 failed. **Live, for real, no skip:** `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` 4 passed against a real throwaway `cactus_rt_*` database on the OVH VPS (run twice - after 033 and again after 034), and `RUN_INBOX_SCHEDULE_GUARDS=1 RUN_INBOX_UPLOAD_GUARDS=1` 24 passed, which is what actually executes the edited `saveDraft` and `insertOutboundMessage` SQL. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md`: NEW **Who a reply goes to**, **Making it look like something** and **Send it and put it to bed** sections; **Writing a new one**, **Sending it later**, **Chasing it up** and **Attachments** rewritten. **Message header rebuilt as two lines** (`unified-inbox`, same uncommitted tree): `ThreadPane.tsx` `MessageHeader` now draws the sender's name and their address on one line and a NEW `ToLine` (`To: …`) under it, with the picture standing beside both. The inbound and outbound arrows are gone from the header, as are the words "Received from"/"Sent to" - kept for a screen reader as `.sr-only`. NEW `components/admin/inbox/AddressLine.tsx` (client): clips an address with an ellipsis and shows the core `AdminTooltip` with the whole of it ONLY when it is actually clipped, measured with a `ResizeObserver`. `.uin-msg-head` no longer wraps, so the time, the dots and the reply arrow can never drop onto a row of their own again; new `.uin-msg-head-lines`, `.uin-msg-head-line`, `.uin-msg-head-to`, `.uin-msg-dir-label`, `.uin-msg-address`, `.uin-msg-address-text` in `styles.tsx`, head avatar 1.5rem -> 2rem. `ComposerOpen.tsx` `ComposerSlot` scrolls the writing box into view when Reply/Reply all/Forward opens or switches it (not on the way in, where a saved draft opens it and the pane has its own opening position). **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Reading oldest first, a conversation now opens on the message worth reading rather than at the top** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. No schema, route, permission or API change - one new client component, two edited components, one settings hint. NEW `modules/unified-inbox/components/admin/inbox/ScrollToMessage.tsx`: a client island that draws nothing and takes `threadId` + `targetId`. It walks OUT from the message for the thing that actually scrolls (first ancestor whose computed `overflow-y` is `auto`/`scroll`; null means the window - the reading pane scrolls its own contents at >=900px and the whole page scrolls below it), asks whether `.uin-thread-head` is `position: sticky` before subtracting its height (pinned only at >=900px, and aligning to a scroller with an opaque band across it hides the message's own header), and then corrects `scrollTop`. **Corrects repeatedly, not once:** an email's HTML lives in a frame that reports its height back and keeps reporting it as pictures arrive (`MessageBody`, `OPENING_HEIGHT = 400`), so a thread of large messages settles a second or two after it is drawn and a single scroll on mount lands nowhere near. A `ResizeObserver` on `.uin-thread-body` re-aligns; `SETTLE_MS = 6000` and the first `wheel`/`touchstart`/`keydown`/`pointerdown` stop it for good. Deliberately NOT listening for `scroll` - that is what the correction itself fires. **Server decides which message**, because it turns on whether the conversation was unread when it was opened and that stops being true one line later: `InboxPanel` captures `wasUnread` BEFORE `setThreadRead(thread.id, false)` and computes `openOnMessage` = null when `settings.newestFirst` (newest is already at the top), else the newest `direction === 'in'` message when it was unread, else the last message of any kind - an internal note included. `ThreadPane` gains the `scrollToMessageId` prop, a `messageDomId()` helper, an `id` on every `<article className="uin-msg ...">`, and renders the island keyed on `thread.id` inside `.uin-thread-body` - suppressed when a draft is opening the writing box, since somebody returning to a half-written reply came for the box. `CollectingPanel` hint for **Show the newest message at the top of a conversation** reworded (its old selling point, not scrolling to find what was last said, is now what the OFF setting does too). Module README "Reading and replying" and core wiki `Unified-Inbox.md` -> "Which way round a conversation reads" both updated. **Gates:** `tsc --noEmit` and `eslint .` clean, whole repo.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**An individual inbox can be shared with named colleagues, and the rail grew a TEAM INBOXES group** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. **NO schema change and no migration** - `uin_inbox_access` already had `(inbox_id, user_id, can_reply)`; it was simply never consulted on an individual inbox. **ACCESS RULE, `lib/access.ts`.** `decideInboxAccess` on `kind = 'individual'` now: no owner -> `manage` fallback (unchanged); owner -> as before; **anybody else -> in only if they hold a row**, with `reply = row.canReply && (canReply || canManage)`. `manage` is still NOT a way past that list, which is the point of the kind. The four call sites that did `inbox.kind === 'individual' ? [] : await listInboxAccess(id)` all now read the rows. `audienceForSave` keeps the owner (forced `canReply: true`, de-duped) and KEEPS the entries the form sent beside them; `defaultUserIds` is still forced to `[owner]` - a guest covering somebody's post is not moved into their inbox. `access.test.ts` rewritten for the new branch, 3 new `audienceForSave` cases. **URL, `lib/list.ts`.** `inbox` param gains three scoped forms - `sent:<inboxId>`, `drafts:<inboxId>`, `mentions:<inboxId>` - parsed by NEW `readFolder()` into NEW `InboxParams.folderInboxId`; `inboxId` explicitly excludes them, or `sent:in1` reads as an address called "sent:in1". `sent:` with nothing after it degrades to the plain folder. `searchableScope` unwraps a scoped folder to its address. `splitInboxes` now takes `meUserId` and returns a THIRD group, `team` - individual inboxes whose `ownerUserId` is not the reader's, orphans included (an address whose owner's account has gone is nobody's, so it is not "Yours"). `default-inbox.test.ts` updated + 2 new cases; `list.test.ts` + 5 new parse cases. **SQL, `lib/db.ts`.** NEW `mentionScope(userId, inboxId)` using the existing `inboxMatch` (so a merged conversation is in both addresses' folders); `listMentions` gains `inboxId?`, `countMentions`/`mentionStatusCounts` gain a third/second arg and now `JOIN uin_threads t`. `draftScope` gains `includeUnfiled` (off for a scoped folder, or the reader's own chat drafts turn up in a colleague's Drafts); `listDrafts`/`countDrafts` thread it through. **UI.** `NavRail.tsx`: `TabInbox` gains `ownerUserId` + `ownerName`; NEW **Team inboxes** group, each row a `.uin-rail-branch` of a `<button class="uin-rail-twist" aria-expanded aria-controls>` beside the link (outside it - a control inside a link cannot be pressed without following it), opening a `.uin-rail-sub` `<ul>` of Sent / Drafts / Mentioned. Mentioned is omitted where `ownerUserId` is null. Open state is client `useState` plus a derived override from `current`, so a deep link to `sent:<id>` arrives expanded with no effect to keep in step; it is deliberately NOT in the query string. **"Asked me" renamed "Mentioned"** in the rail and in `viewTitle`. `styles.tsx`: `.uin-rail-branch`, `.uin-rail-twist` (18px, `rotate(90deg)` at `aria-expanded="true"`, `prefers-reduced-motion` honoured), `.uin-rail-sub` (indent + hairline, and `.uin-rail-sub[hidden] { display: none }` because the group's `display:flex` otherwise beats the `hidden` attribute), plus a lying-down variant under 1200px. `InboxPanel.tsx`: `folderAsked` / `folderInbox` / `folderIds` / `folderOwnerId` / `folderOwnerName` resolved once - **the id is checked against `visible` rather than trusted, and an id that is not on the list yields `[]` rather than falling back to everything**, which would be the E17 breach wearing a folder's name; `currentTab` gains the `folder:id` forms; `viewTitle` gains a "Name &middot; " prefix; the head now draws a title over a colleague's Mentioned tabs. `MentionListView.tsx` gains `ownerName` / `canSettle` - rows read "Marcus asked Sam", the empty state changes, and `MentionActions` is not rendered at all on somebody else's list (the route settles by user id regardless, so the buttons would have been refused). `InboxesPanel.tsx`: the individual branch of **Who can read it** is now a real guest list titled **Who else can read it**, owner excluded from the ticks and stated above them; list row gains a "Shared with N" chip and says so in its meta. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, `npm test` 5654 passed / 421 files. **Live SQL run for real, both PASS, no skip:** `npm run test:inbox-mentions` 9 passed (NEW case: an ask list narrowed to one address, and another person's narrowed list not handing back the first person's), and `RUN_INBOX_SENT_GUARDS=1 vitest run modules/unified-inbox/lib/sent-list.live.test.ts` 10 passed (NEW `describe('the drafts folder')`, 4 cases, incl. the scoped shape that turns the unfiled half of the clause off) - both against real throwaway `cactus_rt_*` databases on the OVH VPS. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md`: the "no guest list on an individual inbox" claim rewritten, NEW **Covering somebody's post** section, and the rail table corrected - it had been calling the SHARED group "Team inboxes", which is now a different group.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**The remote-picture notice shrunk to 10px, and a message that has been shown its pictures keeps them** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. No schema, route, permission or API change - one new pure module, one component, one stylesheet rule. NEW `modules/unified-inbox/components/admin/inbox/remote-images.ts`: `parseShownIds` / `addShownId` (pure, newest-first, deduped, `SHOWN_LIMIT = 500`) plus thin `hasShownImages` / `rememberShownImages` wrappers over `localStorage` key `uin-shown-images`, both reads and writes in try/catch - a private window that cannot store costs one more click, not an error. Split pure-from-storage exactly as `column-widths.ts` is split from `ColumnResizer.tsx`, so the arithmetic is testable in the default node environment; NEW `remote-images.test.ts`, 8 cases. **Kept in the browser and NOT on the server, deliberately:** what is remembered is a fetch THIS machine has already made, which is the exact scope of the promise the notice makes, so there is no column, no migration and no round trip on the way into a conversation. `MessageBody.tsx` gains a `restored` flag - the record is read in an effect keyed on `messageId` (never during render: the server has no `localStorage`, and deciding the frame's `src` from it on the first render is a hydration mismatch), and the notice, the iframe AND the silence timer all wait that one tick, which is what stops the message being loaded twice, once without pictures and again with them. The `setState`-in-effect disable carries the same one-line justification `ColumnResizer` already uses. Pressing **Show pictures** now also calls `rememberShownImages(messageId)`. The notice div gains `.uin-remote-note` (`font-size: 10px; line-height: 1.5`) in `styles.tsx` next to `.uin-frame`, overriding `.alert`'s `var(--text-base)`; the button beside it keeps `.btn-sm`'s own size. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md` "Reading email safely" gains the paragraph. **Gates:** `eslint` clean on all four files, `vitest run` on the new test 8 passed. `npx tsc --noEmit` reports **two errors that are not this task's** - `lib/campaigns/message.ts:143` and `lib/campaigns/runner.ts:498` both missing `bcc`, from another session's in-flight `migrations/033_bcc.sql` work in this same shared worktree.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**A staff member's own phone number, a site dialling code, and the call modal rebuilt** - core + `unified-inbox` + `twilio`, uncommitted working tree. **CORE SCHEMA, two columns.** `User.phone TEXT NULL` - this person's own number, E.164, set on their account page. Deliberately NOT encrypted, unlike `smsOtpPhoneEncrypted` beside it: it is a contact detail rather than a credential, it is shown back to its owner in full, and a per-install-key column is unreadable after a restore into a fresh site. `SiteConfig.diallingCode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '+44'` - which country a number typed without one belongs to. Init migration edited in place AND new idempotent `prisma/core-reconcile/034_staff_phone_and_dialling_code.sql` (latest is now 034, not 033). NEW `lib/phone.ts` - pure, isomorphic, no DB: `toE164(input, diallingCode)` and `isE164`. Handles `+44…`, `0044…`, a single trunk `0`, and bare national digits; the ONE guess is bare digits that already start with the dialling code and leave >= 6 after it, which are read as international (a UK national number written without its trunk zero never starts 44). NEW `lib/phone.test.ts`, 25 cases. NEW `lib/phone.server.ts` - `siteDiallingCode()` + `DEFAULT_DIALLING_CODE`, alone in its own file exactly like `lib/config/timezone.server.ts`, because it touches Prisma and the arithmetic is wanted in the browser too. `app/api/account/profile/route.ts` GET now returns `phone` and `diallingCode`; PATCH takes `phone`, normalises it, 400s in plain English on nonsense, and returns the stored value so the box can show what was actually saved. `AccountPageClient.tsx` gains the Profile > **Your phone number** field (dirty tracking, unsaved-changes guard). `app/api/admin/config/route.ts` Patch gains `diallingCode` with a Zod transform accepting `+44` / `44` / `0044` and storing `+44`; `ConfigPageClient.tsx` shows it in the General > locale grid, which changes from `1fr 1fr 1fr` to `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(100%, 11rem), 1fr))` - the `min(100%, …)` form on purpose, a bare `minmax(11rem, 1fr)` blows out on a phone. **PADDING.** NEW `.admin-content--tight` in `app/globals.css` (`var(--space-4) var(--space-3)`, half of `--space-8`/`--space-6`; `--space-2` on mobile, half of `--space-4`), applied by `AdminShell` on the Inbox route via NEW exported `isInboxRoute` in `lib/nav/sidebar-rail-routes.ts` (`isSidebarRailRoute` now calls it). `app/cactus-admin/inbox/page.tsx` TabStrip margin 1.5rem -> 0.75rem. **`unified-inbox`:** `CallView.tsx` rebuilt - `defaultCallMeAt` / `diallingCode` / `accountHref` props, **Call me at** prefilled from `User.phone` and still editable, both number boxes tidied to E.164 `onBlur` (silently left alone when unreadable - never rewrite a half-typed number under the cursor), plain-English refusals naming both formats. `InboxPanel.tsx` reads `prisma.user.findUnique({phone})` + `siteDiallingCode()` in the same `Promise.all`, only when there is a number to call from. `app/api/calls/route.ts` re-runs `toE164` server-side and dials the normalised pair - the browser's tidy-up is a courtesy, not a guarantee. `styles.tsx`: NEW `.uin-field-row--stack` (label top-aligned, control column-flow, hint wraps and stays visible under 600px) used by all three call rows - the old layout put a nowrap sentence BESIDE the input, which is why **Call me at** was a centimetre wide; and `.uin-field-control input/select/textarea:focus-visible` now underlines (`box-shadow: inset 0 -2px 0`) instead of inheriting the `.uin-modal …:focus-visible` outline box, which on a borderless full-width field landed on the hairlines above and below it. **`twilio`:** `lib/click-to-dial.ts` uses core's `toE164` with the site dialling code for the two TYPED numbers and drops its `normalisePhone` import; refusal copy rewritten. `normalisePhone` in `lib/verification.ts` is deliberately UNCHANGED and still strict - everywhere else in that module a number arrives from Twilio already in E.164, and loosening a webhook-facing validator to help a keyboard is how a wrong number gets dialled. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint` clean on every file this task touched, `npm test` 5638 passed / 420 files, `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` **4 passed** against real throwaway Postgres with all ten module schemas built. **`eslint .` across the whole tree is RED for a reason that is not this task:** another session's in-flight edit put backticks in a comment at `modules/unified-inbox/components/admin/inbox/styles.tsx:1338`, which ends the CSS template literal - the same trap logged two entries below. **Round-trip collision worth knowing:** the unified-inbox live suites provision `cactus_rt_uinint_*` / `cactus_rt_uinmrg_*` and `dropStaleTestObjects` drops EVERY `cactus_rt_%` object, so a concurrent run in another session deletes the round-trip's databases mid-test - it failed three times with `57P01` / "database does not exist" before a clear window let it pass. Nothing to do with the schema change.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Conversations can be merged into one, across addresses and across channels** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. NEW migration `modules/unified-inbox/migrations/031_thread_merges.sql` (another session took 032 for mentions - check `migrations/` before picking a number). **Schema:** `uin_threads.merged_into_id` (self FK ON DELETE SET NULL + partial index) marks the losing side, which is KEPT rather than deleted, exactly as `uin_people.merged_into_id` already does; `uin_messages.merged_from_thread_id` and `uin_drafts.merged_from_thread_id` (FK -> `uin_threads` SET NULL, partial indexes) stamp what a merge MOVED; NEW table `uin_thread_inboxes` (`thread_id`, `inbox_id`, PK on both, CASCADE both ways) is the set of addresses a merged conversation belongs to - EMPTY on every unmerged thread, and populated with the winner's own inbox as well as the losers', so the rule downstream is "rows here if any, `inbox_id` if none" rather than a union that would double-count; NEW table `uin_thread_merges` (`winner_id`, `loser_id`, `user_id`, `snapshot` jsonb, `undone_at`, `undone_by`) with NO foreign keys out, same reasoning as `uin_person_merges`. Both new tables added to `cactus.module.json` teardown - `uin_thread_inboxes` ahead of `uin_threads`, which `lib/teardown.test.ts` enforces. Types stay inside the backup serialiser's set (text / jsonb / timestamp(3)). **ACCESS CHANGE, the one to know about:** `ThreadShape` in `lib/access.ts` gains `absorbedInboxIds`, `threadAccessKind` now asks `effectiveInboxIds(thread).length > 0` rather than `thread.inboxId`, and both `canOpenThread` and `canUserOpenThread` return true if the reader can view ANY participating address. Merging across two addresses therefore WIDENS who can read the merged conversation - chosen deliberately (a merged conversation half the people on it cannot open is not one conversation), and the confirm dialog names every address before it happens. **Threading:** `ThreadRef` and `ThreadCandidate` in `lib/threading.ts` gain `absorbedInboxIds`; `pickThread` and the heuristic branch both go through the new `belongsToInbox()` instead of `===` on `inboxId`. This is what makes a merge survive the next reply - without it the side whose thread was merged away finds nothing to join and 020's split runs again. `threadsForMessageIds` and `candidateThreads` both now exclude `merged_into_id IS NOT NULL`: a merge deliberately LEAVES duplicate copies on the loser (they would collide with three unique indexes on the way across), and following one would file a reply onto a hidden thread. **NEW pure module `lib/thread-merge.ts`** - `pickWinner` (oldest wins, ties on id, so the same set merges the same way whatever order it was ticked), `effectiveInboxIds`, `belongsToInbox`, `mergedInboxIds`, `mergedStatus` (an open half makes the whole thing open), `mergedUnread`, `validateMerge`, `widenedAccessWarning`, `listSentence`, `mergeSummary`, `MAX_MERGE_SOURCES = 20`. 24 unit tests in `lib/thread-merge.test.ts`. **`lib/db.ts`:** new `ABSORBED_INBOX_IDS` SQL fragment and `inboxMatch()`; `visibilityClause` and `filterClauses` both go through `inboxMatch` so a merged conversation shows in EVERY address's tab, and `filterClauses` pushes `t."merged_into_id" IS NULL` for every list, count and status tally in one place; `unreadCounts` LEFT JOINs `uin_thread_inboxes` so it counts once under each address. New `threadsForMerge`, `mergeThreads`, `undoThreadMerge`, `undoableThreadMerges`, `getThreadMerge`, and the private `recomputeThreadCounters` / `recomputeThreadInboxes`. `ThreadRow` and `ThreadDetail` gain `absorbedInboxIds` + `mergedIntoId`; `ThreadListRow` gains `createdAt` (the merge screen needs to name which conversation wins) and `absorbedInboxIds`. `ThreadEventKind` gains `'unmerged'`. Merged-away threads excluded from `unresolvedThreads`, `threadsNeedingLinks`, `providerWatermarks`, `providerThreadsNeedingPeople`, `threadsDueForRetention`, `retentionDueCounts`, `countThreadsForPerson` and `listPeople`'s thread_count. **`providerThreadState` now FOLLOWS the pointer** - the module's own id for a chat stays on the side that lost the merge, so reading the row straight would hand the customer's next chat message to a hidden conversation and it would never appear. **Three GDPR/retention holes closed in the same change:** `deleteThreads` now also deletes messages whose `merged_from_thread_id` is in the set (otherwise erasing somebody leaves their words readable on another person's merged conversation) AND deletes threads merged INTO the ones being deleted (`merged_into_id` is ON DELETE SET NULL, so an orphaned loser would stop being merged away and reappear holding nothing but duplicates); `storedObjectsForThreads` and `exportMessagesForThreads` both follow `merged_from_thread_id`, the export attributing a moved message back to the conversation it came FROM; `personErasePreview` counts conversations with `FILTER (WHERE merged_into_id IS NULL)` so it agrees with the person's own page. **Merge mechanics:** one transaction (`timeout: 60_000`), losers processed ONE AT A TIME so the second loser's copy is weighed against a winner that has already taken the first's. A message the winner already holds is LEFT on the loser rather than deleted - three unique indexes decide that (`(connection_id, thread_id, message_id_header)`, `(thread_id, internal_key)`, `(thread_id, provider_message_id) WHERE source='provider'`) and deleting would make the undo a lie. Drafts likewise on `(thread_id, author_user_id)`. Duplicate record links ARE deleted (the linker puts them back), matching `mergePeople`. Chains are flattened: merging something that has itself absorbed others re-points every thread pointer aimed at it AND the `uin_thread_merges` rows naming it as winner - both halves or neither, since a thread pointing at the new winner whose merge record still names the old one is a merge whose undo hunts for its messages on a conversation they have left. Both sets of ids go in the snapshot. **Provenance across a nested merge was the subtle one:** the message move writes `merged_from_thread_id = COALESCE(src.merged_from_thread_id, loser)` rather than assigning, so a message an EARLIER merge moved keeps the conversation it actually started on - overwriting it made the inner merge un-undoable and exempted those messages from their own person's erasure, which finds them by that column. Undo therefore works off exact `messageIds` / `draftIds` in the snapshot rather than a WHERE on the column (a rule cannot tell this merge's rows from an earlier merge's riding along on the same conversation), and clears the column only `WHEN merged_from_thread_id = loser`. **NEW routes:** `app/api/threads/[id]/merge/route.ts` POST `{loserIds}` and `app/api/threads/merges/[id]/undo/route.ts` POST, both `unifiedinbox.manage`; the merge route checks `canOpenThread` on EVERY thread involved and returns the same refusal whether a thread is missing or unreadable. NEW `ThreadMergeBody` in `lib/validation.ts`. **UI:** `ThreadListView` gains a **Merge** button in the bulk bar (2+ picked, `canManage` only), a `mergePlan` memo using `pickWinner`/`widenedAccessWarning`, and a `ConfirmDialog`; NEW `components/admin/inbox/MergedFrom.tsx` ('use client') renders under `ThreadActions` with **Separate it again** per merge; `ThreadPane` gains `merges` and `otherInboxNames` props; `InboxPanel` loads `undoableThreadMerges` only when `canManage`. New styles `.uin-merged-from*` - tokens only, no hex. **NEW core script `npm run test:inbox-merge`** -> `modules/unified-inbox/lib/thread-merge.live.test.ts` (`RUN_INBOX_MERGE_GUARDS=1` + OVH creds; a skip is a FAIL), 18 cases against a real throwaway Postgres - none of this SQL is executed by `tsc`, `eslint`, `npm test` or the module build gate. **TRAP WORTH RECORDING:** `dropStaleTestObjects` in `lib/backup/vps-database.ts` drops EVERY `cactus_rt_*` database, not only the ones its own run created, so two agents running live suites against the OVH VPS at the same time destroy each other's throwaway databases mid-run - it surfaces as `57P01 terminating connection due to administrator command` or "Server has closed the connection" partway through applying the core schema, which reads exactly like a broken migration and is nothing of the kind. Check `SELECT datname FROM pg_database WHERE datname LIKE 'cactus_rt_%'` and the postgres log before believing it. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, `vitest run modules/unified-inbox` 803 passed / 0 failed. Wiki updated: new "Merging conversations into one", the permissions table, "Who can see what" and the people section.)

One table `uin_mentions` (`id`, `thread_id`, `message_id`, `user_id`, `by_user_id`, `note`, `status` open|snoozed|done, `snooze_until`, `settled_at`, timestamps), UNIQUE `(thread_id, user_id)` - one job per person per conversation, so being asked twice REOPENS the first rather than stacking a second. Snoozed asks wake in `InboxPanel` (beside `wakeDueThreads`) and in the housekeeping cron. Two-leg by contract - `DialRequest.callMeAt` is required - and a refusal is `{ ok: false, reason }` rather than a throw. `firstDialler` is the CHEAP question (database only, for deciding whether to draw a button); `callerNumbers` reaches the telephony API and belongs to the screen that opens. `twilio` publishes it (`lib/dialler.ts` -> `twilioDialler`, manifest entry `twilio-dialler`, `serverOnly: true`), and the two-leg TwiML that was inline in `app/api/admin/twilio/make-call/route.ts` moved to `lib/click-to-dial.ts` so the settings screen and the seam place the identical call with the identical caller-ID checks; the route keeps its 503-for-unconfigured / 502-for-thrown split via `result.code`. `twilio` 0.1.34 -> 0.1.35, `requiresCoreVersion` -> 0.5.1534. UNIFIED-INBOX: `ComposeMenu.tsx` ('use client') turns the rail's pen into a split button - the pen still writes an email, the arrow opens Discussion / Call / SMS. The menu is `position: fixed` and placed off the trigger's rect because `.uin-rail-scroll` scrolls (a column at >=1200px, a strip with `overflow-y: hidden` below), and an absolutely-positioned menu inside it loses its bottom half on a phone; it closes on scroll, resize, Escape and any pointerdown outside. Entries are omitted when the site cannot do them, and with none there is no arrow at all. `lib/list.ts` gains `ComposeKind` and `parseComposeKind` - `compose=1` still means the email composer, `discussion`/`sms`/`call` are new, anything else opens nothing (the pre-existing contract, tested). New `components/admin/inbox/ComposeModal.tsx` carries the dialog chrome the three short forms share (focus trap, Escape, scroll lock, the you-have-typed-something question) as a render-prop; `ComposeView` keeps its own, being a screenful rather than a form. DISCUSSION = a thread with `channel = 'discussion'` (migration `030_discussions.sql` widens the `uin_threads_channel_check` by DROP IF EXISTS + ADD; `001_initial.sql` edited in place to match), made of the internal notes the module already had. `createDiscussionThread` opens it UNREAD - the one place it differs from a note, because a conversation carries one unread flag between everybody and a discussion nobody is told about is one nobody reads. `POST /api/m/unified-inbox/discussions` takes `unifiedinbox.view` (a note leaves the building through no door), checks `canViewInbox` for EVERY named address BEFORE writing anything, and starts one discussion per address - one thread cannot honestly have two guest lists, two unread counts and one `inbox_id`, and this is the same answer `020_internal_threads.sql` already gave. `InboxPanel` forces `canReply = false` on a discussion so the composer opens on the note box rather than a Reply that would refuse. Mention notification extracted to `lib/mentions.ts` and the notes route refactored onto it. SMS = `POST /api/m/unified-inbox/sms` over core's existing `getActiveSmsProvider()` / `normaliseSmsNumber`; CALL = `POST /api/m/unified-inbox/calls` over the new seam. NEITHER WRITES A ROW: the module that owns texts and calls publishes them straight back as a `core.conversation-provider` phone conversation, so a second copy here would be the same message twice with only one of them knowing whether it was delivered. New pure `lib/sms-segments.ts` (160/70, one curly quote shortens the whole message) with 8 tests; `lib/db.ts` gains `latestPhoneOnThread` (inbound only - `from_phone` on our own outgoing message is our own number). CORE FIX en route: `scripts/generate-module-router.mjs` typed `ApiHandlerModule` as a map of handlers, so every route exporting `maxDuration` or `dynamic` was a type error in a generated file nobody can edit - 25 of them, all pre-existing. Now `Record<string, unknown>` with the handler narrowed at the one place it is read. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean (0 errors, down from 25), `eslint .` clean, `npm test` 5594 passed / 0 failed. No new table, column, sequence or type, so no backup round-trip - the only DDL is a widened CHECK constraint, which a fresh restore builds from the migrations anyway.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**An inbox is now either the business’s or one person’s** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. Front's distinction, and the only place on this site where holding `unifiedinbox.manage` is NOT a way past a list. Migration `029_inbox_kind.sql` adds `uin_inboxes.kind` (TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'shared', CHECK IN ('individual','shared')) and `owner_user_id` (TEXT, FK -> `User(id)` **ON DELETE SET NULL**, indexed). No dollar-quoting anywhere - constraints are DROP IF EXISTS then ADD rather than a DO block, because a `$$` pair takes the whole module out of the backup round-trip. **Nothing is converted:** every existing address becomes 'shared', which is what they all already behaved as; turning one into somebody's own is a deliberate act in Settings, since it takes an address away from people who can read it today. `decideInboxAccess` gained a leading `inbox: {kind, ownerUserId}` argument and now branches on the kind BEFORE the manage short-circuit - individual: owner only (and they still need `unifiedinbox.view`), everybody else false including a manager; owner NULL (their account was deleted) falls back to manage so the post is not sealed in. Shared is byte-for-byte the old rule. `canViewInbox` / `canReplyToInbox` / `canUserViewInbox` / `canUserReplyToInbox` lost their manage short-circuits and read the new `getInboxAudience(id)`; `visibleInboxIds` / `replyableInboxIds` share a new private `audienceIndex()` (`listAllInboxAccess` + new `listInboxAudiences()`, two queries for the whole site, never two per address). New pure `audienceForSave()` forces an individual inbox's guest list to exactly its owner - written redundantly to `uin_inbox_access` AND `uin_user_default_inbox` so any query that reads the guest list and has never heard of `kind` still gets the right answer. New `lib/inbox-kind.ts`: pure `kindProblem()` (individual needs an owner; **individual may not be the catch-all**, because unplaceable post filed into a private mailbox is invisible to the person who has to sort it out), `ownerProblem()` (refuses a deleted or suspended account) and `settleIndividualAudience()`, called by BOTH inbox routes straight after the row is written - the guest list arrives as a second request and that request can fail. The PATCH route judges the kind on the MERGE of before+patch, so turning on the catch-all without mentioning the kind is refused from either end; the access PUT re-applies `audienceForSave` so a stale screen cannot put the old team back on a personal inbox. `inboxIsSomebodysOwn` is now a UNION ALL of the default-inbox row and `kind='individual'`. UI: new **Whose inbox it is** radio group in `InboxesPanel` with an owner picker, the guest-list group replaced by a sentence on a personal one, the catch-all tick hidden, a Personal/Shared chip on every list row. `TabInbox` gained `kind`; new pure `splitInboxes()` in `lib/list.ts` (built on `pinDefaultInbox`: an individual inbox is under Yours by its nature, a pinned shared one by preference) and the rail's second group is now **Shared inboxes** - only those drag. A thread in an individual inbox offers only its owner in the **With** menu. 12 new tests across `access.test.ts`, `inbox-kind.test.ts` and `default-inbox.test.ts`. `npm run typecheck` unchanged at 25 pre-existing errors in generated `lib/modules/router.ts` (verified against a clean tree), `eslint modules/unified-inbox` clean, 739 module tests pass - the single failure in `list.test.ts` is ANOTHER session's in-flight `parseComposeKind` work in the shared module worktree, not this change. **Backup gate not run:** the round-trip needs the OVH credentials exported and the server quiet; the schema-coverage backstop is green and the migration adds TEXT columns only, which it already has a branch for.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**The Inbox gets the width the sidebar was using** - core, uncommitted working tree. The `/inbox` screen is core's own host for `core.inbox-tabs`, so this is a core behaviour and no module name appears anywhere in it - the unified-inbox panel just happens to be the tab that wanted the room. `isPuckEditorRoute` is still what decides the `admin-content--puck` padding class; the Inbox keeps ordinary padding. Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Reviews go to Google, and Google asks your customers for one** - `google-shopping-for-shop` + `reviews-for-shop`, uncommitted working tree. Two halves of Google's review programme, and neither needs a core change.

**The review feed.** `reviews-for-shop` gains `lib/published-reviews-provider.ts`, registered on a new vendor-neutral extension point **`shop.product-reviews`** (`reviewsPublishedReviewsProvider({limit, offset})`): published reviews of ACTIVE, non-catalogue-hidden products, newest live first, ordered by `COALESCE(published_at, created_at) DESC, id ASC` so paging cannot repeat or skip a row, each carrying `ratingMax`, `verifiedPurchase`, `invited` (a `LEFT JOIN rvw_invites` on order+product, `skipped_reason IS NULL` so an order the nightly job deliberately left alone never reads as one it wrote to) and the order number. No email, no IP, no shop reply. It is a PUBLISHER, so gsf never imports it - the same optional-companion seam `lib/delivery-timing.ts` already uses, which is what keeps the build green on a site without the reviews module.

gsf's side: `lib/reviews-source.ts` (registry lookup, per-row validation, paged read capped at 5000/500 - and the paging follows a page on what the provider RETURNED, not on what survived checking, or one malformed review would truncate the feed), `lib/review-feed-xml.ts` (pure renderer for Google's product reviews schema **2.3**, written off the XSD itself: every complexType there is an `xs:sequence`, so element ORDER is load-bearing, and `nonEmptyStringType` means an absent value leaves the element out rather than writing an empty one; C0 control characters are stripped because one of them in one review invalidates the whole document), `lib/review-feed-data.ts` (brand/GTIN/MPN off `gsf_product_data` and the product's own barcode, product URLs through shop's `productUrl`, publisher name and favicon from core's `resolveBranding` - and a product EXCLUDED from the product feed has its reviews left out too). SKUs are deliberately never sent, the same line the product feed holds.

**The address.** `/google-shopping/feed.xml?key=<token>&content=reviews`, on the existing route. Core dispatches the literal segment `feed.xml` and nothing else (`app/(public)/[slug]/feed.xml/route.ts`), so a second public file name would have meant a core change; a query parameter is what Merchant Center's scheduled fetch takes anyway. The reviews branch skips the price test (reviews quote no prices, so a quote-only shop may publish them) and has its own switch, `reviews_feed_enabled`.

**Google Customer Reviews.** New Puck block **`GoogleCustomerReviews`** on layout type `shopConfirmation` (`components/CustomerReviewsBlock.tsx` + `.rsc.tsx` + `CustomerReviewsOptIn.tsx`), auto-placed on first install. The RSC half reads settings and whether the site's banner carries a `marketing` category; the client half reads `orderNumber` and the signed receipt token `t` off the URL, fetches **`GET /api/m/google-shopping-for-shop/public/customer-reviews`** and renders Google's `surveyoptin` dialog. Order of operations is load-bearing: `window.renderOptIn` is defined BEFORE `apis.google.com/js/platform.js?onload=renderOptIn` is appended, or the callback fires against nothing. `window.__cactusGcrRendered` stops two copies of the block asking twice.

The payload is built server-side (`lib/customer-reviews.ts`) and returns null - the same empty answer for every refusal, so probing cannot tell the cases apart - unless the token verifies against that order (shop's `verifyOrderReceiptToken`), the order is PAID and a Merchant Center account number is set. The estimated delivery date is `paidAt ?? createdAt` plus the slowest line's handling + transit days from the delivery-timing provider, falling back to the shop-wide `customer_reviews_delivery_days`, counted Monday-Friday (`addWorkingDays`, bank holidays deliberately not modelled). GTINs ride along where known. The email leaves the server only to the confirmation page of the order it belongs to, and the answer is `no-store, private`.

**Migrations:** `005_product_reviews_feed.sql` (`reviews_feed_enabled`) and `006_customer_reviews_optin.sql` (`customer_reviews_enabled`, `customer_reviews_style`, `customer_reviews_delivery_days`), both `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`. BOOLEAN, TEXT and INTEGER only, so `isSupportedUdtName` needs no new branch. **Manifest:** `cspOrigins` script `https://apis.google.com`, img `ssl.gstatic.com`/`www.gstatic.com`/`www.google.com`, frame `www.google.com`/`apis.google.com`.

**Existing installs place the block by hand.** `autoPlaceBlocks` runs once, behind `layoutsSeededAt`, so a site that already has this module will not have the marker put on its confirmation layout - the settings tab says so in as many words.

**Gates:** `npm run typecheck` and `eslint` clean; 55 module tests green, including a new `review-feed-xml.test.ts` (element order asserted, not assumed), `reviews-source.test.ts` (13 seam cases) and `customer-reviews-dates.test.ts`. The provider's SQL was RUN against the live Deskwell database read-only - `tsc`, `eslint` and the build gate all pass raw SQL Postgres would reject, so it was executed rather than eyeballed.)

Last updated: 2026-09-05 (**Both CSV exports ask which columns you want first** - `shop` and `shop-variations`, uncommitted working tree. The Products **Export CSV** button and the Variations **Download variations CSV** button were plain `<Link>`s to a route that always wrote every column; an owner who wanted product code, categories and nothing else got a 48-column file to prune by hand. New shared client component **`modules/shop/components/admin/ExportColumnsModal.tsx`** - generic, knows nothing about either format: it takes `groups`, a `storageKey`, an optional `requiredKeys`, and a `buildHref(keys)` that turns the ticks into a download url. The choice is kept in **localStorage** (`shop.products.export.columns`, `shop-variations.export.columns`), read once in the `useState` initialiser rather than an effect so the boxes do not visibly re-tick, and every read and write is in a try/catch with 'everything ticked' as the fallback - it is a per-person habit, not shop configuration, so no table and no migration. Shop side: `buildExportCsv(rows, columns?)` gained an optional subset (defaults to `CSV_COLUMNS`, so the Google-Sheet mirror and every other caller are untouched), `GET /api/m/shop/admin/products/export` now reads `?columns=` through the existing `pickCsvColumns` - unknown names dropped, empty falling back to the whole format, the same lenient rule the import-template route uses - and `lib/csv.ts` gained **`CSV_COLUMN_LABELS`** (a human name per column) and **`CSV_COLUMN_GROUPS`** (nine groups: the basics, prices and VAT, stock, catalogue, words and pictures, size and weight, pre-orders, downloads, related and upsells). A filtered sheet still re-imports as long as `sku` or `slug` is in it - `headerMatchesUpdateFormat` is satisfied - which is asserted rather than assumed. Variations side is harder because half its header is dynamic: `Option N`/`Value N` grow with the widest product and the variant-field providers append their own columns, neither knowable without building the whole grid (which IS the export). So new client-safe **`modules/shop-variations/lib/export-columns.ts`** offers the 16 fixed columns by name plus **two group tokens**, `__options__` and `__extras__`, one tick each; `exportVariationsCsv(columns?)` builds a **`kinds` array positionally alongside the header** and filters on that, never on the label, so a provider column called 'Price' can never switch the real Price column. **`Variant ID` is a required tick** - it is what the importer and the Sheet Pull match a row back on, and without it a renamed option value reads as a different variant. It cannot live in `lib/csv.ts` (that file imports Prisma and the picker is a client component), which is also why `PRICE_TYPE_COLUMNS` is repeated there and pinned to the real header by a test. New tests: 4 in `modules/shop/lib/csv-roundtrip.test.ts` (groups cover `CSV_COLUMNS` exactly once, a label for every column, subset written in format order not caller order, empty falls back to all) and 6 in new `modules/shop-variations/lib/csv-export-columns.test.ts` (the fixed list matches what the exporter actually writes, groups cover fixed + the two tokens, cells stay aligned with the filtered header, the two blocks come whole). `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, 5351 tests green. No schema, no SQL, no migration. Wiki: `Shop.md` and `Shop-variations.md` both updated.)

Last updated: 2026-09-05 (**Half of what the build uploads was four databases we do not use** - core working tree. Measured against a real build's own file traces: 261 functions, 462 MB of distinct files, and **29.8 GB written across the functions**, which is the pile Vercel hashes and uploads in "Deploying outputs" - 80-120s of every deploy. 14 GB of that, 46.9%, was `@prisma/client`'s base64-inlined WebAssembly query engine AND query compiler for **all five** dialects it supports - CockroachDB, MySQL, SQL Server, SQLite and PostgreSQL - copied whole into all 258 functions at 53 MB each. None of it can run here: the wasm client is for Edge and for driver adapters, and `prisma/schema.prisma` declares neither (no `driverAdapters` preview feature, no `engineType`), no route sets `runtime = 'edge'`, and `proxy.ts` is Node-only by design. Every query is served by the native engine, `node_modules/.prisma/client/libquery_engine-<platform>.node`, which is traced separately. New **`outputFileTracingExcludes`** in `next.config.ts`, keyed `'**'` (route globs, `contains` semantics, same matcher as `outputFileTracingIncludes` - see `next/dist/build/collect-build-traces.js`), drops `query_engine_bg.*.wasm-base64.*`, `query_compiler_bg.*.wasm-base64.*` and `.prisma/client/query_engine_bg.wasm`. Replayed against the existing traces: 5,418 entries and 13.97 GB gone, the native engine still present in all 258 functions, payload 29.8 GB -> 15.8 GB. Separately, new leaf module **`lib/media/worker-url.ts`** holds `workerUrl()` - an environment-variable read with no imports of its own, which had been sitting in `lib/media/upload.ts` alongside `sharp`, `@aws-sdk/client-s3`, the glTF optimiser (`draco3d`, `@gltf-transform`) and `jsdom` via `lib/sanitize`. `lib/media/asset-token.ts`, which signs the media urls on **every public page**, imported it from there and dragged the lot along; it now takes the leaf. `upload.ts` re-exports `workerUrl` so all existing callers are untouched. **This does not by itself take sharp out of the public functions**: `lib/modules/extension-points.public.ts` also imports `product-attributes-for-shop`'s `option-source-provider`, which statically imports `generateSwatchCopies` -> `lib/media/renditions.ts` -> `sharp`, so the 2 GB that path is worth needs a module-side change as well. `tsc --noEmit`, `eslint .`, client-graph check and 5,332 tests all clean.)

Last updated: 2026-09-04 (**A guest can open their own order by giving the delivery postcode, and every order email links to it** - `shop`, uncommitted working tree. A shopper who checked out without an account had no order page at all: no tracking, no invoice, and no way to correct the company name their accounts department had just queried - all of which arrived at the shop by telephone instead. They now prove themselves with the **delivery postcode**, typed any way at all (`normalisePostcode` strips everything that is not a letter or a digit, so 'E1 1AA', 'e1 1aa' and 'E11AA' are one postcode and 'B29 7QB' is not), and the **order number** likewise (`orderNumberCandidates` accepts 'DW000172', 'dw000172', '000172' and plain '172' against the shop's own `orderNumberPrefix`, and offers what was typed as a candidate too for a shop numbering its own way; `findOrdersByNumberCandidates` in `lib/db/orders.ts` looks them all up at once and refuses to guess when two are real). New pure libs: `lib/order-lookup.ts` and `lib/order-tracking.ts` (`signOrderTrackingToken`/`verifyOrderTrackingToken` in their own `track:` namespace, `orderTrackingRootSlug`, `orderTrackingBasePath`, `orderTrackingPath`, `orderTrackingUrl`), both with unit tests. Proof is kept in a signed httpOnly cookie **`cactus_shop_order_access`** (`lib/guest-order-access.ts` - HMAC over a list of order ids plus a signed expiry, 30 days, at most 10 orders, newest first; declared in the manifest's new `cacheBypassCookies` so a page carrying one is never made shareable). Who may see an order is now ONE rule in `lib/order-viewer.ts` (`orderViewerFor` + `resolveOrderViewer`), asked by the page, the receipt and all six routes through `lib/order-route-access.ts` (`requireOrderAccess`) - member-and-owner, or guest-with-cookie, and everything else is the same 404. `getMemberOrderDetail` splits into `loadOrderDetail(orderId)` plus a thin ownership wrapper. A verified guest gets **exactly** what a member gets: `app/public/shop/account/orders/[id]/page.tsx` renders the postcode gate in place when nobody has proved themselves, then the full page outside `MemberAccountShell` with a 'Track another order' back link and an inline account offer (`components/public/GuestOrderAccountOffer.tsx`, core's `RegisterForm` prefilled with the order email, behind the existing `postPurchaseAccountPrompt` switch); the receipt page and `api/member/orders/[id]` (GET+PATCH), `/billing`, `/pay`, `/pay/confirm`, `/requests` and `api/member/requests/[id]` all go through `requireOrderAccess`. `withdrawRequest` is now scoped to the **order** rather than the member, which also fixes a member who registered after ordering being unable to withdraw their own request. New pages `app/public/shop/track-order/page.tsx` (exports `TrackOrderPageView`) and `app/public/shop/track-order/[orderNumber]/page.tsx` (the email's landing page: signed-in owner or proved guest is redirected straight to the order, a genuine token names the order and asks only for the postcode, anything else falls back to the plain form), new client `components/public/OrderAccessForm.tsx`, server `components/public/OrderAccessGate.tsx`, and `components/public/track-order-css.ts` (`TRACK_ORDER_CSS`, `sot-*` prefix, injected `<style>`, every colour a token). New public route **`POST /api/m/shop/public/orders/track`** - one wording for every failure so failures cannot be used to find real order numbers. Brute force is defended by a new table **`shp_order_access_attempts`** (`migrations/038_order_access_attempts.sql`, mirrored in `001_initial.sql`, added to the manifest `teardown`) and `lib/db/order-access.ts`: eight wrong postcodes on one order locks it for an hour, the count resets after an hour of quiet, and it is keyed on the ORDER because the existing in-memory IP limiter is per-instance and keyed on something the guesser chooses. New config: **`guestOrderTrackingEnabled`** (default true) and **`orderTrackingRootSlug`** (default `track-order`), both on Settings -> Shop -> Checkout -> Order history; the root slug is claimed through `lib/root-slug.ts` and `app/root/[slug]/page.tsx`, which now branches tracker-first before the product page, and is added to `lib/robots.ts` alongside `/shop/track-order`. Emails: new merge tags **`orderUrl`/`hasOrderUrl`** on all 13 customer-facing order templates with a default 'Keep track of your order at ...' line; filled centrally in `notifyOrderCustomer` (so every email AND every SMS carries it and none can be missed) plus `orderTrackingVars` in `lib/email.ts` for the two that send direct - the credit note and the reissued invoice. **Owner copy overrides do not gain the new line automatically** - an edited template keeps its own wording and needs `{{orderUrl}}` adding by hand. Verified against real Postgres: new `modules/shop/lib/db/order-access.live.test.ts` (9 tests, added to `npm run test:ledger-guards`) exercises the lockout arithmetic, the window reset, the sweep, the candidate lookup and the order-scoped withdraw; `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` passed.)

Last updated: 2026-09-04 (**The customer's own order page rebuilt around what they came for** - `shop` v0.1.392, released. `app/public/shop/account/orders/[id]/page.tsx` had grown one `Section` at a time - pay online, proforma, invoice address, purchase order number, items, parcels, downloads, totals, refunds, addresses, requests - into eleven identical `.card` blocks in one column, every one the same size and weight, with the paperwork strung off the 'Placed 3 August 2026' line as middot-separated links (five wrapped lines on an order with two invoices and a credit note). Rebuilt in priority order: header (number, status badge, date, item count, total), then a **four-step progress rail** (Ordered, Paid, Dispatched, Complete), then anything still to DO (money owed, an open cancel/return request) as tinted callouts, then the receipt - items AND totals in ONE card - then everything else as **paired cards, two columns on desktop and one on a phone**. New files, all in `modules/shop`: `components/public/order-detail-css.ts` (`ORDER_DETAIL_CSS`, `sod-*` prefix, injected `<style>` exactly like `order-confirmation-css.ts` - never a core `globals.css` edit); `components/public/OrderDetailChrome.tsx` (`Icon` + `ICON_TICK`/`ICON_CLOCK`/`ICON_ALERT`/`ICON_IMAGE`/`ICON_DOC`/`ICON_PRINT`/`ICON_DOWNLOAD`, `OrderCard` with `title`/`note`/`flush`/`wide`/`foot`, `OrderNote` with a required `info|ok|warn|bad` tone); `components/public/OrderProgressRail.tsx`; `components/public/OrderItemList.tsx`; `components/public/OrderDocuments.tsx` (exports the `OrderDocument` type the page builds its paperwork list from); and `lib/order-progress.ts` (`orderProgressSteps`, `orderStopped`) - pure, derived every render like the dispatch summary, never stored, and it marks everything before the first unfinished step done so the rail can never show a finished step after an unfinished one. `lib/order-display.ts` gains `formatOrderDateShort` ('3 Aug') for the rail. `components/public/OrderRequestPanel.tsx` swapped its own `.card`/`.card-title` chrome for `sod-card`/`sod-card-head`/`sod-card-body` so it matches the rest of the page (it is used on this page only). Three behaviour changes rather than pure restyling: **grouped lines now sit together** on this page (`sortLinesByGroup`/`effectiveGroup`, as the cart, checkout, confirmation and order emails already did - a desk and its drawer unit were the last two lines on the platform still printed unrelated); **the unit price shows where quantity is more than one** ('GBP 240.00 x 4' instead of leaving the reader to do the arithmetic); and **state and action stopped sharing a row** - 'Dispatched' and 'Buy again' were two pills of equal standing, now the badges sit left and the button right. The `.card` spacing bug went with it: globals' `.card` carries `margin-bottom: var(--space-6)` and every one of the old sections sat in a `gap: var(--space-4)` grid, so every gap on the page was the two stacked. Cancelled and refunded orders get a sentence instead of a rail (there is no next step to point at); `ON_HOLD` gets a callout above the rail. Colours are all tokens, light and dark; two physical breakpoints (620px pairs to one column, 480px drops the item price under the name), plus a `@media print` block and a stacked rail under 560px. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint .` clean, 909 shop tests green - the 5 skips are `lib/db/refund-netting.live.test.ts`, which is live-database gated and untouched by this. No schema, no SQL, no migration.)

Last updated: 2026-09-04 (**A refund taken before the order is invoiced is left OFF the invoice instead of credited** - `shop`, uncommitted working tree. A line goes out of stock while the order is being picked, the money goes back, and only later does the order reach whatever the shop invoices on: there is nothing to credit, because nothing was invoiced. Previously the invoice went out for the whole basket, so the customer got a VAT invoice for goods they never had and the shop declared output tax on money it had already returned - and the order screen offered **Raise the credit note** against an invoice that did not exist. **The invariant now is that every refund is dealt with exactly once: netted off an invoice, or credited off one, never both and never neither.** New pure `lib/invoice-net-of-refunds.ts` (`netOrderOfRefunds`) takes settled refund lines off an order before `buildInvoiceMoney` sees it - returns the SAME object identities when there are no refunds, so the overwhelming majority of invoices are byte-for-byte what they were. Per line it works on the CHARGED gross (`total * (1 - discountRatio)` plus tax where EXCLUSIVE - the discount ratio matters, because `prepareRefund`'s cap is on the UNdiscounted value and the money that actually went back is the discounted one), takes the refund off it, drops the line when nothing is left and scales `total`/`taxAmount` together otherwise; quantity can legitimately land at 0 where somebody held part of the money back on every unit, and that line stays because the money is still the shop's. Delivery and its tax survive untouched (`order.taxAmount` less the ORIGINAL goods tax), the discount scales by what is left of the goods, and the total is recomputed rather than subtracted. **New migration `037_refund_netted_off_invoice.sql`** (001_initial edited to match) adds `shp_refunds.netted_off_invoice_id` + index. It exists because DATES CANNOT ANSWER THIS: a refund can be PENDING when the invoice is raised and settle seconds later, so it is older than the invoice and yet was not taken off it, and it needs a credit note like any other. `lib/db/refunds.ts` gains `listUncreditedRefundLines(orderId, alsoNettedOffInvoiceId?)` (COMPLETED, no credit note row, not already netted - the second argument lets ONE invoice's own marks back in, which is exactly what the reissue path needs so a replacement comes out at the figures the original did), `markRefundsNettedOff` and `clearRefundsNettedOff`; `ShpRefund` gains `nettedOffInvoiceId`. `buildInvoiceInsertInput` now returns `{ input, nettedRefundIds }` (**breaking for any caller** - `issueInvoiceForOrder` and `lib/invoice-reissue.ts` both updated) and marks them once the invoice row exists; `voidInvoiceAndTellSinks` clears them, since the document that absorbed them is gone. `issueCreditNoteForRefund` refuses a netted refund in plain English, and its no-invoice refusal now reads as "nothing to credit" rather than an error. Admin credit-note GET carries `nettedRefundIds` and `invoiced`; `OrderDetailScreen` splits the uncredited list three ways - netted (a sentence, no button), settled-before-any-invoice (a sentence, no button), genuinely missing (the button, as before). 10 new tests in `lib/invoice-net-of-refunds.test.ts`. **The SQL is executed for real** by new `lib/db/refund-netting.live.test.ts` (5 tests, gated on `RUN_LEDGER_GUARDS`/`RUN_BACKUP_ROUNDTRIP`, throwaway `cactus_rt_shopnet_*` database on the OVH VPS, core init SQL applied first because shop's migrations reach for `Layout`) - added to the `test:ledger-guards` script. `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint` clean, 5293 tests green, `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` a real PASS (4 tests, 108s).)

Last updated: 2026-09-04 (**The tracking link shows on the customer's own order page** - `shop` v0.1.386, released. One `Track` anchor per parcel now, labelled `Track your parcel` for a single parcel and `Track parcel N` where there are several, `target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"`, `--color-primary`. **The http/https check moved out of `lib/order-status.ts` into new `lib/tracking-url.ts`** (`safeTrackingUrl`): two surfaces now render this value as an href and neither should take "the writer validated it" on trust - the dispatch route still refuses anything else on the way in, this is the same rule on the way out for rows written before that check existed, imported, or set by hand. 3 new tests. **Note for whoever reads the history:** ANOTHER SESSION was given this same task in parallel and wrote the release notes that now sit on tag v0.1.386, which points at this session's commit `d901093`; their own attempt is not in the tree. It now returns a `DispatchDetails` with `trackingUrl` and `trackingLinks` as well. **The awkward bit is that one `{{trackingUrl}}` is one anchor and three parcels are three**, so both are offered: `trackingUrl` is filled ONLY when there is exactly one distinct link (the ordinary case, and the only one where a lone anchor cannot point at the wrong parcel), and `trackingLinks` is a block of one `<p><a>` per parcel labelled `Track <number>` - or `Track your parcel` where the parcel has a link but no number. Last updated: 2026-09-04 (**Customers can correct who their invoice is made out to, and a company change credits and reissues it** - `shop`, uncommitted working tree. The distinction the whole change turns on is statutory: a moved ADDRESS is the same party being billed for the same supply, so the document is corrected in place; a changed COMPANY is a different legal person, so the invoice is credited in full and replaced. **New migration `036_invoice_billing_changes.sql`** (001_initial edited to match for fresh installs) adds `shp_invoices.customer_amendments` JSONB, `superseded_at`, `superseded_by_invoice_id` (self FK, ON DELETE SET NULL), `supersede_reason`, and **replaces the partial unique index** `shp_invoices_order_issued_key` with `shp_invoices_order_live_key` on `(order_id) WHERE status='ISSUED' AND superseded_at IS NULL` - the old one made a replacement impossible to insert. `insertInvoice` matches BOTH index names on 23505, since an install that has not taken 036 yet still reports the old one. The original stays **ISSUED, not VOID**: void says withdrawn-before-anybody-acted, and with a credit note beside it the books would reverse the same sale twice. New `lib/customer-billing.ts` holds every rule, pure and shared by page and route (`customerBillingEditOffered`, `customerCanEditBilling`, `billingChangeEffect` -> `none`/`order`/`reissue` + `amendsInvoice`, `reissueAvailable`, `reissueWarning`, `normaliseCompanyName`/`normaliseAddress` - case and spacing only, punctuation kept, so "Acme Ltd." is NOT "Acme Ltd"), 21 tests in `lib/customer-billing.test.ts`. New `lib/invoice-reissue.ts` is the one place a change happens: credit note + supersede + replacement invoice go in **one `prisma.$transaction`** (`supersedeInvoice` is guarded on the row still being live, so two simultaneous presses cannot both raise a replacement), sinks and email after the commit. `lib/db/invoices.ts` gains `amendInvoiceBillingAddress` (the ONE writer that edits a snapshot - address only, history captured inside the same UPDATE), `supersedeInvoice`, `linkSupersedingInvoice`, and `getInvoiceForOrder` now excludes superseded rows; `insertInvoice`/`insertCreditNote` take an optional `PrismaTransactionClient` (core's exported type - `Prisma.TransactionClient` does NOT match the extended client). `lib/invoices.ts` extracts `buildInvoiceInsertInput` and `siteTimezone`, exports `buildWording`, adds trigger `REISSUE`; `lib/credit-notes.ts` extracts `buildFullCreditNoteInput` (subtotal is BACKED OUT of the total - the credit note table has no discount column) and `creditWording`, exports `tellTheBooks`. New `lib/db/orders.ts` `setOrderBillingIdentity` (organisation + billing address in one statement; never touches the delivery address). New route `app/api/member/orders/[id]/billing/route.ts`, PATCH, **two-phase**: a company change on an issued invoice comes back `{needsConfirmation, warning}` and only a second request carrying `confirm` goes through. New client `components/public/OrderBillingPanel.tsx`; the account order page now lists EVERY issued invoice (superseded ones marked "(cancelled)") rather than only the live one. New config `customerBillingEditEnabled` and `customerBillingReissueEnabled`, both default false, with their section under Settings -> Shop -> Checkout. New email template `shop.invoice-reissued` / trigger `INVOICE_REISSUED` - one email for both documents, because a bare credit note reads as money coming back. **Both documents travel as attached PDFs and neither is linked in the body**: the credit note is what cancels the invoice the buyer's accounts department has already filed, so `emailTheCustomer` prints it alongside the replacement (`invoiceEmailAttachment` + `creditNoteEmailAttachment` in parallel, each still allowed to come back null) and the default wording gained `hasInvoicePdf` / `hasCreditNotePdf` conditionals. `invoiceUrl` and `creditNoteUrl` are still passed and still offered as tags, so an owner who has edited this email keeps their links working. **`orderCompanyName` changed, and it is the one behaviour change outside the new feature**: the DELIVERY address fallback now only applies to an order carrying no billing address of its own (SQL twin `ORDER_COMPANY_SQL` in `lib/db/orders.ts` matched, verified by running it against the live Deskwell schema read-only). Without it, a customer clearing their company had it handed straight back by the delivery label - and migration 027 backfilled `customer_organisation` out of that very field, so that is every older order. Admin order screen shows a **Replaced** badge and the reason for a superseded invoice, so two ISSUED rows are not left unexplained (`present()` in the admin invoice route carries the two new fields). `ShpInvoice`'s four new fields are **optional on purpose**: the type doubles as the shape a proforma, a credit note and quote-for-shop's own document are presented in, and none of those can be superseded. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint` clean, 5264 tests green, and `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` a real PASS (4 tests, 117s) - though note its harness builds a subset of module schemas that does not include shop, so the shop columns are covered by `lib/backup/schema-coverage.test.ts` rather than by an actual round-trip. The DDL itself WAS executed for real: a throwaway `cactus_rt_*` database on the OVH VPS ran the 023-era table then 036 on top of it twice, the fresh-install shape from 001 then 036, and the amendment UPDATE, the supersede guard and the index still refusing two live invoices - temporary test file, since deleted, database dropped.)

Last updated: 2026-09-04 (**Order emails link each line to its exact product or variation** - `shop`, uncommitted working tree, same session as the entry below. `OrderEmailLine` gains `url`; `renderOrderItemsTable` wraps BOTH the name and the thumbnail in one `<a>` (new `linked()` helper, `color:#333333;text-decoration:underline` - no shop palette exists inside an email, and a hardcoded brand blue would clash with every wrapper that is not blue). New `productEmailUrl(slug, siteUrl, style)` exported from `lib/order-items-email.ts`: absolute via `productUrl`, null when either half is missing, because a relative href resolves to nothing in an inbox. **The variation case needs no special handling and must not get any**: a variant is a catalogue-hidden child product with its own slug, and shop's product page hands an unshowable slug to `shop.product-page-resolver`, which is where shop-variations opens the parent's page on that variation - so the line's OWN slug is already the exact-variation link, and this module never learns how variations are wired. New slim `getProductSlugsByIds` in `lib/db/products.ts` (id + slug only, not `getProductsByIds`' `SELECT *`: these emails are assembled inside a payment webhook). `orderEmailLines` and `renderOrderItemsEmailTable` widen their config Pick to `'currencySymbol' | 'productUrlStyle'`; slug and media reads run in one `Promise.all`, each `.catch`ed to an empty map so a failed read costs the links or the pictures and never the email. `lib/shipment-email.ts` does the same for its two lists and moves `getShopConfigCached()`/`getSiteUrl()` above the line building, since the link needs the URL style. Covers ORDER_CONFIRMED, PAYMENT_RECEIVED, ORDER_PLACED_UNPAID, both ADMIN_NEW_ORDER alerts, PARTIAL_SHIPPED and STATUS_* - every table goes through the one renderer. 6 new tests in `lib/order-items-email.test.ts` (19 total). `tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint modules/shop` clean, 909 shop+email tests green.)

Last updated: 2026-09-04 (**Credit notes travel as a file, and a guard that catches merge tags nothing fills** - `shop`, uncommitted working tree, same session as the three entries below. `lib/invoice-attachment.ts` gains `creditNoteEmailAttachment(creditNoteNumber, config)` beside the invoice one - same dynamic imports, printed on `documentPageSetup('shopInvoice')` because a credit note is drawn on the invoice's layout, filename off `creditNotePdfFilenamePrefix`. New config `creditNoteAttachToEmail` (boolean, default true) with its checkbox under Credit notes, disabled unless the customer email AND PDFs are both on. `emailCustomer` in `lib/credit-notes.ts` takes the config, attaches the file, and passes `hasCreditNotePdf` / `hasCreditNoteLink`: the template prints "attached" when there is a file and the LINK ONLY when there is not, so a shop with PDFs off is not left sending neither. **Also fixed: `{{#if hasReason}}` on shop.credit-note-issued has existed since the template was written and NOTHING has ever passed `hasReason`** - the reason a refund was given had never once printed, because a conditional whose flag is missing drops silently and no check in the repo looked. New `lib/email-templates.test.ts` is that check: for every shop template, every `{{tag}}` and `{{#if flag}}` in its own default subject and body must appear in `mergeTags` (site tags excepted), every `rawTags` entry must be a declared merge tag, and every key must be `shop.`-prefixed. It found EIGHT more templates whose conditional flags were never offered in the editor - `hasCustomerReference`, `hasPreOrderItems`, `hasPaymentInstructions`, `hasPaymentReference`, `hasItems`, `hasAdminNote`, `hasRefund`, `hasCustomerNote` - all now declared. Declaring a flag changes nothing at send (mergeTags only drives the editor chips and preview samples, and a `[hasX]` sample is not 'true' so previews are unchanged); it means an owner rewriting the email can discover the block instead of silently losing it. **Watch the scan trap if you repeat it:** a block boundary taken at the next `key: 'shop.` swallows the COMMENT above the next template, so a comment quoting `{{trackingNumber}}` briefly added it to shop.status-processing's tags - removed. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint` clean, 5245 tests green across two consecutive full runs, 42 new.)

Last updated: 2026-09-04 (**Order emails list what was bought as a photographed table, not one run-on paragraph** - `shop`, uncommitted working tree, same session as the invoice attachment below. Reported as a five-item order arriving as a single unbroken sentence: the item list was a newline-joined plain string, and HTML collapses every one of those newlines to a space, so the text part read correctly and the HTML part - which is what everybody actually sees - did not. New `modules/shop/lib/order-items-email.ts`: `renderOrderItemsTable(lines)` (pure, table-based, every rule inline, no CSS custom properties - core's email constraints, see `lib/email/blocks.ts`), `orderEmailLines(items, config)` (bulk media read, at most one extra query per email), `renderOrderItemsEmailTable` for the two together, and `absoluteImageUrl(url, siteUrl)` - an inbox has no origin, so a site-relative `src` is a broken picture in every client; anything neither http(s) nor `/`-rooted is dropped. Columns earn their place: the picture column appears only when a line has one (blank `&nbsp;` cell for the pictureless lines, or names slide left), the price column only for a priced list. Wired into `lib/order-fulfillment.ts` (ORDER_CONFIRMED / PAYMENT_RECEIVED / ADMIN_NEW_ORDER), `lib/order-placed-email.ts` (ORDER_PLACED_UNPAID / ADMIN_NEW_ORDER_UNPAID) and `lib/shipment-email.ts` (PARTIAL_SHIPPED, which now reads `getOrderItems` purely to get a product id per `orderItemId` - the dispatch summary carries names but no product). **Two things had to change in `lib/email-templates.ts` together:** `rawTags` now names `orderItems` / `dispatchedItems` / `outstandingItems` (core escapes every non-raw tag, so the markup would have arrived as visible angle brackets), and `<p>{{orderItems}}</p>` is unwrapped to bare `{{orderItems}}` - a table may not sit inside a `<p>`. An install whose owner has EDITED one of those bodies keeps their `<p>` wrapper; parsers auto-close it, which is untidy rather than broken. Thumbnail is the LINE'S OWN product's primary image, i.e. the variation's child product - deliberately NOT the parent's, because the parent link lives in `svr_variants`, a shop-variations table this module must not read, and the thank-you page and account order page already resolve it exactly this way. 13 new tests in `lib/order-items-email.test.ts`. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint` clean, 5195 tests green.)

New `modules/shop/lib/invoice-attachment.ts` exports `invoiceEmailAttachment(orderId, config)`, returning an `EmailAttachment` or `null`; new config key `invoiceAttachToEmail` (boolean, default true) sits beside `invoicePdfEnabled`/`invoicePdfFilenamePrefix` in `lib/config.ts`, with its checkbox under Invoices in `components/admin/ShopSettingsTab.tsx` (disabled while PDFs are off). `lib/order-status.ts` attaches it on `COMPLETED` only, and the lookup sits AFTER the `if (changed)` block so an order invoiced on that very transition sends the invoice it has just been given rather than the nothing it had a moment earlier. `getInvoiceForOrder` is ISSUED-only, so a voided invoice is never re-sent; null covers setting off, PDFs off, no invoice raised, and a printer that fell over, and none of them may stop the email. Last updated: 2026-09-04 (**A proforma order now says which half of the dance it is in, and the payment note can go to the supplier's accounts desk** - `purchase-orders` v0.1.30, uncommitted working tree, on top of the same version's document filing. **Status:** reported as "a proforma was added but it still says Sent", and it did.
